Extensive experiments on DTU, LLFF, and Mip-NeRF360 demonstrate that TWINGS consistently outperforms existing methods, delivering detailed and accurate reconstructions under sparse-view scenarios.

</details>


### [147] [JMed48k: A Multi-Profession Japanese Medical Licensing Benchmark for Vision-Language Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22080)
*Yue Xun,Junyu Liu,Qian Niu,Xinyi Wang,Zheng Yuan,Zirui Li,Zequn Zhang,Bowen Zhao,Shujun Wang,Irene Li,Kan Hatakeyama-Sato,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JMed48k是一个包含48,862道题目和20,142张图像的多职业日本医疗执照考试基准，用于评估视觉语言模型。研究发现专有和开源模型能有效利用图像信息，而医学专用模型对视觉证据的利用有限，且不同职业间图像移除效应差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医疗视觉语言模型评估基准通常缺乏多职业覆盖和细粒度视觉内容分析，无法全面反映模型在真实医疗执照考试场景下的表现。作者希望构建一个跨职业、大规模、带有视觉内容标注的日本医疗执照考试基准，以支持可重复的视觉语言模型评估。

Method: 从日本厚生劳动省发布的官方PDF材料中收集2005-2025年间的11个国家执照考试题目，构建包含48,862道题目和20,142张图像的JMed48k语料库，视觉内容按8类分类法标注。从中提取近五年子集JMed48k-Eval（12,484道计分题），并设计配对图像移除审计方法，通过对比移除前后模型答题状态变化，分析模型对视觉信息的利用程度。

Result: 评估了21个模型（专有、开源、医学专用），分别报告纯文本和含图像题目表现。配对图像移除审计显示：专有和开源模型从图像中获益显著，而医学专用系统对视觉证据的利用有限，许多正确回答在移除图像后仍能保持。专有模型中，图像移除的净效应在不同职业间差异达7倍，从医师题目的+5.7分到公共卫生护士题目的+39.8分不等。

Conclusion: JMed48k能够支持跨职业、可重复的视觉语言模型评估。研究表明当前医学专用模型在整合视觉信息方面存在不足，且不同医疗职业对视觉信息的需求差异显著，这为未来医疗视觉语言模型的发展提供了重要方向。

Abstract: We introduce JMed48k, a multi-profession Japanese healthcare licensing benchmark for evaluating vision-language models. Built from official PDF materials released by the Japanese Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare, JMed48k contains 48,862 exam questions and 20,142 images from 11 national licensing examinations between 2005 and 2025, with visual content annotated under an 8-type taxonomy. From this corpus, we derive JMed48k-Eval, a recent five-year evaluation subset with 12,484 scored questions, including 9,905 text-only questions and 2,579 questions with images. We evaluate 21 proprietary, open-source, and medical-specific models, reporting text-only and with-image performance separately. Because these subsets contain different questions, we further introduce a paired image-removal audit that evaluates questions with images before and after removing visual content to explore four answer-transition states. The audit shows that proprietary and open source models gain substantially from images, whereas medical-specific systems show limited observable use of visual evidence, with many correct answers persisting after image removal. Even among proprietary models, the net image-removal effect varies sevenfold across professions, from +5.7 points on Physician questions to +39.8 points on Public Health Nurse questions. We release JMed48k to support reproducible, profession-stratified evaluation of vision-language models in medical licensing settings.

</details>


### [148] [Direct content-based retrieval from music scores images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22255)
*Noelia Luna-Barahona,Antonio Ríos-Vila,Félix Fuentes-Hurtado,David Rizo,Jorge Calvo-Zaragoza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究乐谱图像的内容检索方法，比较了基于OMR的转录方法、无转录Transformer模型和文本提示LLM在四种不同语料库上的表现，发现各方法在不同条件下各有优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乐谱数字化对保存和可访问性至关重要，但目前信息检索主要依赖元数据（标题、作曲家等），基于内容的乐谱图像检索尚未得到充分探索，而这对音乐家、音乐学家和教育工作者具有重要价值。

Method: 首先研究乐谱中哪些特征最相关于检索，定义从标注语料库构建查询数据集的系统方法；然后比较三种方法：基于OMR的转录方法、无转录Transformer模型（直接从乐谱图像识别查询）、以及文本提示的LLM方法，在四种不同特征（数据集规模、图像质量、排版方式）的语料库上进行评估。

Result: OMR流水线在域内检索中表现更好，而无转录Transformer模型在处理域间变化时更有效；各方法在不同条件下各有优势。

Conclusion: 不同检索方法在不同条件下各有所长，OMR方法在域内表现优异，而免转录方法在跨域泛化方面更具优势，为乐谱内容检索提供了多样化的技术路线。

Abstract: The digitization of musical scores plays a crucial role in their preservation and accessibility, yet information retrieval still depends mainly on metadata searches, such as by title or composer. Content based search in music score images remains underexplored compared to text documents, despite its potential value for musicians, musicologists, and educators. This work contributes to the field by first studying which characteristics of a score are most relevant for search and by defining a systematic method to build query datasets from any annotated corpus. We also consider diverse methods for content-based search on music score images, ranging from transcription-based approaches relying on Optical Music Recognition (OMR), to a transcription-free Transformer model trained to recognize queries directly from score images, and a text-prompted Large Language Model. Our experiments evaluate these models on four corpora exhibiting diverse characteristics in terms of dataset size, image quality, and typesetting mechanisms. Overall, each method excels under different conditions: OMR-based pipelines achieve higher in-domain retrieval, whereas transcription-free models handle domain variability more effectively.

</details>


### [149] [PixIE: Prompted Pixel-Space Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23531)
*Ruirui Lin,Guoxi Huang,David Bull,Nantheera Anantrasirichai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixIE是一种由视觉基础模型语义提示的前馈像素空间低光照图像增强框架，通过跨尺度去噪、DINO提示像素块调制、空间-通道压缩和多感受野像素嵌入等技术，在多个基准上显著提升了PSNR和LPIPS指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光照图像存在严重噪声、对比度损失和语义模糊，去噪与细节恢复需联合处理，现有方法难以兼顾两者。

Method: 1) 跨尺度去噪（抑制噪声并保持结构）；2) DINO-Prompted Pixel Blocks (DPPBs) 注入DINOv3特征进行逐像素调制；3) Spatial-Channel Compaction (SCC) 联合缩减空间token网格和通道维度；4) Multi-Receptive-Field Pixel Embedding (MRPE) 提供邻域感知的像素表示。

Result: 在LLIE基准上，PSNR提升1.9-15.0%，LPIPS降低8.5-44.4%，细节更锐利、纹理更稳定。

Conclusion: PixIE在重建保真度和感知质量上均优于现有方法，证明了语义提示与像素空间处理结合的有效性。

Abstract: Low-light images suffer from severe noise, contrast loss, and semantic ambiguity, making enhancement a joint problem of denoising and detail recovery. We propose PixIE, a feed-forward pixel-space LLIE framework semantically prompted by a vision foundation model. PixIE first performs cross-scale denoising to suppress noise and preserve structure, then refines details using DINO-Prompted Pixel Blocks (DPPBs), which inject intermediate DINOv3 features through patch-conditioned, spatially continuous per-pixel modulation. To make pixel-space attention efficient across scales, we introduce Spatial-Channel Compaction (SCC), which jointly reduces the spatial token grid and channel dimension. We further propose Multi-Receptive-Field Pixel Embedding (MRPE) to provide neighborhood-aware pixel representations before semantic prompting, improving robustness to signal-dependent noise beyond point-wise embeddings. Experiments on LLIE benchmarks show that PixIE improves average PSNR by 1.9-15.0% over recent state-of-the-art methods and reduces LPIPS by 8.5-44.4%. Qualitative comparisons further show sharper details and more stable textures, improving both reconstruction fidelity and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [150] [Nano World Models: A Minimalist Implementation of Future Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23993)
*Siqiao Huang,Partha Kaushik,Michael Chen,Hengkai Pan,Kaiwen Geng,Omar Chehab,Fernando Moreno-Pino,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Nano World Models，一个基于扩散强迫（Diffusion Forcing）的极简代码库，用于未来视频预测。它统一了生成目标、模型规模、行动条件机制、潜在观测空间、数据集、评估协议和长程rollout流程，支持对世界模型各组件进行可控研究。实验覆盖简单控制环境、游戏模拟和真实机器人数据，分析了预测参数化、架构规模、行动注入、采样预算和领域复杂度对视频预测质量及自回归rollout行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管工业级交互式视频生成发展迅速，但研究社区仍缺乏紧凑、可复现且易于扩展的实现来研究现代世界模型的设计选择。现有实现中各组件的设计常纠缠在一起，难以进行独立可控研究。

Method: 构建基于扩散强迫（Diffusion Forcing）的极简代码库Nano World Models，提供统一的接口涵盖生成目标、模型规模、行动条件机制、潜在观测空间、数据集、评估协议和长程rollout流程，并通过多领域实验（简单控制、游戏模拟、真实机器人数据）系统分析各设计因素对视频预测的影响。

Result: 通过跨不同领域的实验，揭示了预测参数化、架构规模、行动注入方式、采样预算和领域复杂度对视频预测质量和自回归rollout行为的具体影响。

Conclusion: Nano World Models为开放、可复现、科学化的世界模型研究提供了一个紧凑且可扩展的实验基础，通过开源代码、配置、评估脚本和预训练检查点促进社区研究。

Abstract: World models have become a central paradigm for learning predictive simulators that support generation, planning, and decision-making. Yet, despite rapid progress in industry-scale interactive video generation, the broader research community still lacks compact, reproducible, and easily extensible implementations for studying the design choices underlying modern world models. We introduce Nano World Models, a minimalist codebase for future video prediction centered around diffusion forcing. Nano World Models provides a unified interface for generative objectives, model scales, action-conditioning mechanisms, latent observation spaces, datasets, evaluation protocols, and long-horizon rollout procedures. This design enables controlled studies of world-modeling components that are often entangled across separate implementations. Through experiments across simple control environments, game simulation, and real-robot data, we examine how prediction parameterization, architecture scale, action injection, sampling budget, and domain complexity affect video prediction quality and autoregressive rollout behavior. By releasing code, configurations, evaluation scripts, and pretrained checkpoints, Nano World Models aims to provide a compact yet extensible experimental substrate for open, reproducible, and scientific world-model research.

</details>


### [151] [Coarse-to-Fine Domain Incremental Learning with Attentive Distillation for Mining Footprint Segmentation in Multispectral Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24460)
*Alif Tri Handoyo,Vincent C. S. Lee,Rizka Widyarini Purwanto,Alex M. Lechner,Deanna Kemp,Muhamad Risqi U. Saputra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MineC2FNet，一种粗到细的域增量学习框架，利用丰富的粗粒度标注数据提升细粒度采矿足迹分割性能，通过师生架构和注意力蒸馏机制减轻域偏移，并在219张精细标注的数据集上取得超越现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用遥感和深度学习自动绘制全球采矿足迹面临细粒度标注数据稀缺的瓶颈，而现有的粗粒度数据虽然丰富但存在显著的域偏移问题，难以直接用于提升细粒度分割性能。

Method: 提出MineC2FNet粗到细域增量学习框架，采用师生架构，在特征层和预测层引入注意力蒸馏机制，选择性迁移粗域中的通用知识，同时利用有限的细粒度数据进行边界精炼。

Result: 在包含219张图像、覆盖不同地理区域和矿产类型的精细标注数据集上，MineC2FNet在与域适应和域增量学习等方法的对比中取得最优性能，有效处理了域偏移问题。

Conclusion: 粗到细的域增量学习策略能够有效利用现成的粗标注数据来提升细粒度采矿足迹分割任务，为全球采矿监测提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Automatically mapping and segmenting global mining footprints using remote sensing and deep learning is critical for monitoring the socio-environmental risks and impacts of mining, yet its progress is hindered by the scarcity of fine-grained annotated data. Although large-scale datasets with coarse boundaries are widely available, leveraging them to improve fine-grained segmentation is challenging due to significant domain shift. To address this, we propose MineC2FNet, a coarse-to-fine domain incremental learning framework that exploits abundant coarse data to enhance fine-grained mining footprint segmentation. MineC2FNet adopts a teacher-student architecture with attentive distillation at both the feature and prediction levels, selectively transferring generalized knowledge from the coarse domain while enabling boundary refinement using limited fine-grained data (fine domain). We further introduce an expertly validated dataset of 219 images with precise boundary annotations across diverse geographies and commodities. Extensive experiments against state-of-the-art approaches, including domain adaptation and domain incremental learning methods, demonstrate that MineC2FNet achieves superior performance while effectively handling domain shift. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/risqiutama/MineC2FNet.

</details>


### [152] [SRUG: Shadow-Guided Relightable Urban Scene with Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24700)
*Yonghao Zhao,Zexin Yin,Jian Yang,Beibei Wang,Jin Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SRUG框架，利用阴影引导3D补全模型恢复不可见区域几何，结合迭代材质分解和基于物理的光照模型，实现城市场景的可重光照，在视图合成和重光照任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市场景重光照存在两个核心挑战：(1) 场景无界且存在大量不可见区域，但这些不可见区域会向可见区域投射阴影；(2) 稀疏视图和复杂光照条件导致材质分解存在严重歧义。

Method: (1) 阴影引导的3D补全模型，用于恢复不可见区域几何，生成物理合理的阴影；(2) 迭代材质分解方案，利用大型材质模型(LMM)提供材质监督并逐步分解场景材质属性；(3) 基于物理的照明模型，捕捉城市场景复杂光照并支持可靠重光照。

Result: 大量定量评估和视觉比较表明，SRUG在新视角合成和重光照任务上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SRUG通过阴影引导补全和迭代材质分解有效解决了城市场景重光照中的不可见区域遮挡和材质歧义问题，实现了更可靠的重光照效果。

Abstract: Creating relightable urban scenes from images or videos is widely useful but highly ill-posed. Urban environments are typically unbounded and extend beyond the visible regions. As a result, many portions of the scene remain unobserved, yet these invisible regions can cast shadows onto visible areas. Reasonably modeling shadows cast by such invisible regions is challenging and poses a significant obstacle to creating relightable urban scenes. At the same time, sparse input views and complex illumination conditions further complicate relighting, as they introduce severe ambiguities in material decomposition. In this paper, we propose Shadow-guided Relightable Urban Scene with Generation model (SRUG), a novel framework designed to address relighting challenges in urban scenes. SRUG leverages shadows to guide a 3D completion model for recovering the geometry of invisible regions, promoting the synthesis of physically reasonable shadows. In addition, SRUG employs an iterative material decomposition scheme that applies the large material model (LMM) to provide material supervision and iteratively decompose the scene's material properties, enabling robust material decomposition. Building upon these components, we introduce a physically-based lighting model that captures the complex illumination of urban scenes and supports reliable relighting. Extensive quantitative evaluations and visual comparisons demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches in both novel view synthesis and relighting tasks.

</details>


### [153] [VEOcc: Voxel-Centric Online Semantic Occupancy Prediction For Embodied Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25059)
*Ruoyu Wang,Yong Liu,Sheng Tao,Yuhang Lin,Yukai Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VEOcc提出了一种基于体素的在线3D占用预测与建图框架，采用递归感知-同化范式，无需初始尺度估计即可实现高效地图扩展，并通过时空感知在线更新策略（TLA、RCM、CSU）鲁棒聚合噪声时序观测，在Occ-ScanNet和EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet上达到SOTA性能，且具备零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯的方法在结构边界保真度上表现不佳，且严重依赖预定义的场景大小先验，限制了自主探索中的操作效率。作者希望消除对初始尺度估计的需求，实现高效、开放的在线地图扩展。

Method: 提出体素中心框架VEOcc，采用递归感知-同化范式；提出时空感知在线更新策略，包含跨时间逻辑聚合（TLA）保持时序一致性、可靠性感知置信度调制（RCM）校准空间不确定性、置信度驱动增量状态更新（CSU）实现鲁棒全局状态同化。

Result: 在Occ-ScanNet和EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet上达到SOTA性能；在自收集视频序列的零样本评估中展现出在完全未见过的真实环境中的鲁棒分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: VEOcc为自主探索提供了一种准确且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Crucial for autonomous exploration, online 3D occupancy prediction and mapping incrementally constructs dense spatial representations on the fly. However, recent Gaussian-centric methods struggle with structural boundary fidelity and rely heavily on predefined scene-size priors, fundamentally limiting their operational efficiency. In this work, we present VEOcc, a voxel-centric framework formulated as a recursive perception-and-assimilation paradigm. By eliminating the need for initial scale estimation, VEOcc enables highly streamlined, open-ended map expansion. Furthermore, to robustly aggregate noisy temporal observations within the discrete voxel space, we propose a Spatio-Temporal-Aware Online Update Strategy. It integrates Cross-Temporal Logit Aggregation (TLA) for temporal consistency, Reliability-Aware Confidence Modulation (RCM) for spatial uncertainty calibration, and Confidence-Driven Incremental State Update (CSU) for robust global state assimilation. % Extensive experiments on Occ-ScanNet and EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet demonstrate that VEOcc establishes new state-of-the-art performance in both local and embodied settings, providing an accurate and efficient solution for real-world exploration. Extensive experiments on Occ-ScanNet and EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet demonstrate that VEOcc establishes new state-of-the-art performance in both local and embodied settings. Notably, zero-shot evaluations on self-collected video sequences further confirm its robust out-of-distribution generalization capability in completely unseen real-world environments. Ultimately, our framework provides an accurate and highly efficient solution for autonomous exploration. Code and supplementary visualizations are available on our project page: https://wryzju.github.io/VEOcc/.

</details>


### [154] [A Principled Self-Referenced Early Stopping Approach for Deep Image Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25299)
*Chaoyan Huang,Cheng-Han Huang,Ismail R. Alkhouri,Rongrong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对深度图像先验（DIP）在逆成像问题中因过参数化导致的过拟合问题，本文提出一种基于伪自参考图像构建的早期停止检测框架，利用两个独立噪声副本的观测结果推导出三种具体算法，在无需精确噪声估计的情况下， outperforms 现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DIP在解决逆成像问题时存在过拟合噪声测量的缺陷，而现有基于运行方差波动的早期停止方法会出现过早波动导致重建不稳定，因此需要更鲁棒的过拟合检测机制。

Method: 首先理论证明当存在两个独立噪声副本时可实现近乎最优的早期停止；基于此观察，构建伪自参考图像作为替代，提出三种针对不同逆成像问题的算法；同时提供单参考验证、伪验证估计以及共享噪声影响的理论分析支撑。

Result: 在自然图像恢复和医学图像重建等多种逆成像问题中，面对不同噪声水平和噪声类型，所提方法均一致优于现有DIP早期停止方法，且无需精确估计噪声水平。

Conclusion: 通过伪自参考图像构建实现对DIP过拟合的有效检测，拓宽了DIP在无训练数据场景下的实际应用可靠性。

Abstract: Recently, Deep Image Prior (DIP) has demonstrated strong capabilities for solving inverse imaging problems (IIPs) by optimizing a randomly initialized convolutional neural network in a training-data-free regime. However, DIP suffers from overfitting to noisy measurements due to network over-parameterization, making early stopping (ES) essential. The most successful ES method tracks fluctuations in the running variance of the network output to detect overfitting. However, in many applications, these fluctuations may appear prematurely, leading to unstable reconstructions. In this paper, we first show that nearly optimal DIP early stopping can be achieved when two independent noisy copies of the degraded image are available. Motivated by this observation, and since obtaining two fully independent copies is infeasible, we propose an overfitting detection framework based on constructing pseudo self-referenced images, resulting in three IIP-specific algorithms. Our approach is further supported by theoretical results on single-reference validation, pseudo-validation estimation, and the impact of shared noise. Across different IIPs, ranging from natural image restoration to medical image reconstruction, and under varying noise levels and noise types, our methods consistently outperform existing DIP early stopping approaches, all without requiring an accurate estimate of the noise level.

</details>


### [155] [Paris 2.0: A Decentralized Diffusion Model for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26064)
*Ali Rouzbayani,Bidhan Roy,Marcos Villagra,Zhiying Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Paris 2.0是首个通过去中心化计算预训练的视频生成模型，在低分辨率文本到视频训练中，相比同数据同算力的集中式模型，FVD从561.04降至279.01（提升约2倍），同时提升了CLIP文本-视频相似度和美学评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决去中心化训练下时序连贯视频生成这一悬而未决的问题，证明无需单一GPU集群即可训练视频生成模型。

Method: 基于Paris 1.0（去中心化扩散模型）的训练方案，扩展到视频生成领域，采用去中心化计算进行预训练。

Result: 在低分辨率文本到视频任务中，相比集中式模型：FVD从561.04降至279.01（约2倍提升），CLIP文本-视频相似度和美学评分均有所提升。

Conclusion: Paris 2.0成功证明了去中心化训练能够实现视频生成，且性能优于同等条件下的集中式模型。

Abstract: We present Paris 2.0, the first video generation model pre-trained through decentralized computation. Its training recipe builds upon Paris 1.0 (arXiv:2510.03434), the first ever open-weight Decentralized Diffusion Model (DDM), which showed that image generation can be trained without a monolithic GPU cluster. However, temporally coherent video generation had remained an open problem under decentralized training, and Paris 2.0 closes it.
  In low-resolution text-to-video training, against a monolithic model trained on the same data under a matched total compute budget, Paris 2.0 cuts Frechet Video Distance (FVD) from 561.04 to 279.01, a ~2.0x improvement, and lifts CLIP text-video similarity and aesthetic score.

</details>


### [156] [Reinforcing Few-step Generators via Reward-Tilted Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26108)
*Yushi Huang,Xiangxin Zhou,Ruoyu Wang,Chi Zhang,Jun Zhang,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RTDMD，一个两阶段框架，通过将分布匹配蒸馏与奖励引导的强化学习统一，实现了仅需4步推理即可在偏好、美学和组合性指标上达到SOTA的少步文本到图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步扩散蒸馏模型虽然实现了高效图像生成，但将其与人类偏好对齐仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出两阶段框架RTDMD：第一阶段引入AC-DMD，通过子区间分布匹配和一致性正则化实现环境一致的分布匹配蒸馏；第二阶段联合优化分布匹配项和奖励最大化项，其中奖励最大化项采用混合策略梯度（结合GRPO风格估计器和直接奖励反向传播），并引入SubGRPO降低方差。

Result: 在SD3、SD3.5和FLUX.2上，RTDMD仅用4步推理即在偏好、美学和组合性指标上超越此前所有少步文本到图像生成方法，达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过将分布匹配蒸馏与奖励引导的强化学习统一，RTDMD有效解决了少步扩散生成模型与人类偏好对齐的难题。

Abstract: Recent advances in few-step diffusion distillation have enabled efficient image generation, yet aligning these models with human preferences remains challenging. We propose Reward-Tilted Distribution Matching Distillation (RTDMD), a two-stage framework that unifies distribution matching distillation with reward-guided reinforcement learning for few-step flow generators. We show that minimizing the KL divergence to a reward-tilted teacher distribution naturally decomposes into a distribution matching term and a reward maximization term. In the first stage, we introduce Ambient-Consistent Distribution Matching Distillation (AC-DMD), which performs subinterval-wise distribution matching and augments the fake score objective with a consistency regularizer to help the fake score model track the shifting generator distribution under limited updates. In the second stage, we jointly optimize both terms: for the reward maximization term, we derive a hybrid policy gradient that combines a GRPO-style estimator for the stochastic intermediate transitions with direct reward backpropagation through the deterministic final step, and further introduce step-subset GRPO (SubGRPO) to reduce variance. Experiments on SD3, SD3.5, and FLUX.2 demonstrate that RTDMD establishes new state-of-the-art results across preference, aesthetic, and compositional metrics with only 4 inference steps, outperforming previous few-step text-to-image generation methods. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Harahan/RTDMD.

</details>


### [157] [ChartAct: A Benchmark for Dynamic Chart Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26994)
*Muye Huang,Lin Wu,Lingling Zhang,Hang Yan,Zhiyuan Wang,Yumeng Fu,Zesheng Yang,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了ChartAct，一个面向动态图表理解的交互式基准测试，包含673个动态图表和1,440个问答样本，评估了11个多模态模型，发现最强模型Claude-Opus-4.7成功率为84.5%，多数模型低于60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表理解基准主要关注静态图表，但真实世界的图表往往是动态和交互式的，关键信息需要通过悬停、点击、缩放、拖拽等操作才能获取，因此亟需一个能够评估模型动态图表理解能力的基准。

Method: 从8个真实图表网站收集并筛选了673个动态图表，覆盖7种常见图表类型，构建了1,440个高质量问答样本。每个样本在动态图表和仪表板图表两种环境中实例化，以评估不同上下文下的动态图表理解能力。

Result: 系统评估了11个先进多模态模型和GUI智能体，最强模型Claude-Opus-4.7平均成功率为84.5%，大多数模型低于60%，表明现有模型在动态图表理解方面仍存在明显局限性。

Conclusion: ChartAct为研究真实交互环境中的图表理解提供了新基准，通过详细的失败归因和案例分析揭示了现有模型的不足。

Abstract: Charts are widely used to present complex data for analysis and decision making. Existing chart understanding benchmarks mainly focus on static charts, but real-world charts are often dynamic and interactive. Key information may only appear after actions such as hovering, clicking, zooming, or dragging. Dynamic chart understanding therefore requires models to identify visible content, choose proper interactions, and reason over changing chart states. To evaluate this ability, we propose ChartAct, an interactive benchmark for dynamic chart understanding. ChartAct collects and filters 673 dynamic charts from 8 real chart websites, covers 7 common chart types, and constructs 1,440 high-quality question-answer samples. Each sample is instantiated in two environments, Dynamic Chart and Dashboard Chart, to evaluate dynamic chart understanding under different contexts. Based on ChartAct, we systematically evaluate 11 advanced multimodal models and GUI agents. Experimental results show that existing models still have clear limitations in dynamic chart understanding. The strongest model, Claude-Opus-4.7, achieves an average success rate of 84.5\%, while most models remain below 60\%. We also conduct detailed failure attribution and case analysis. ChartAct provides a new benchmark for studying chart understanding in real interactive environments. Codes at https://github.com/wulin-wulin/OSWorld_Chart

</details>


### [158] [Structure over Pixels: Learning Variable-Length Visual Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27696)
*Piotr Wyrwiński,Kacper Dobek,Krzysztof Krawiec*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STROP是一种离散视觉分词器架构，能够形成结构化场景表示并同时学习每张图像应使用的视觉程序长度，通过四阶段课程学习和DINOv3特征监督，使得程序长度随场景复杂度增长，并涌现出组合结构特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应分词器要么需要事后搜索，要么在预训练的离散速率集合中选择，无法学习与模型和场景耦合的连续每图序列长度，且通常针对像素重建进行训练，强调纹理而非结构。

Method: 提出STROP架构，包含专用长度头，通过四阶段课程学习（使用冻结DINOv3特征的局部率-失真探针监督）来估计单次前向传播中的有效前缀长度，绕过像素级重建梯度，完全基于高层潜在表示质量塑造码本。

Result: 程序长度随场景复杂度增长；在下游密集预测迁移和直接检查学习到的码本词汇中，均出现了组合结构的迹象。

Conclusion: STROP能够学习与场景复杂度匹配的可变长度结构表示，并且在不依赖像素重建的情况下，通过高层语义特征引导码本学习，可形成具有组合性的结构化场景表征。

Abstract: Discrete visual tokenizers translate images into ordered sequences of codes, providing a natural representation for structural description of scenes. Yet existing adaptive tokenizers either require post-hoc search or select among a discrete set of pre-trained rates, rather than learning a continuous per-image sequence length coupled to the model and scene, and they typically train against pixel reconstruction, emphasizing texture rather than structure. We propose STROP, a discrete visual tokenizer architecture that forms structural scene representations and simultaneously learns how long an image's visual program should be. Using a four-phase curriculum supervised by local rate--distortion probes against frozen DINOv3 features, STROP optimizes a dedicated length head that estimates the active prefix length in a single forward pass. By bypassing pixel-level reconstruction gradients, the codebook is shaped entirely by the quality of higher-level latent representations. Program length grows with scene complexity, and signs of compositional structure emerge both in downstream dense-prediction transfer and in direct inspection of the learned code vocabulary.

</details>


### [159] [ROVER: Routing Object-Centric Visual Evidence for Grounded Multi-Image Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27959)
*Guannan Lv,Ren Nie,Hongjian Dou,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ROVER，一种轻量级可学习插件，通过路由面向对象的视觉证据，实现高效的全局视觉证据选择，解决多模态大语言模型在定位推理中存在的场景理解弱化和计算开销问题。在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上集成并经过SFT-to-GRPO训练后，在MM-GCoT和VideoEspresso基准上取得最优性能，并展现出强迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于定位的MLLM方法存在两个主要局限：(1) 注入裁剪图像块或RoI特征会削弱整体场景理解和物体间关系；(2) 自适应视觉特征选择需要细粒度监督或复杂启发式规则。同时，解码成本随RoI数量和尺寸线性增长。

Method: ROVER是一种轻量级可学习插件，在每次物体定位预测时注入步骤特定的token三元组，实现：(i) 聚合当前推理上下文；(ii) 通过面向对象的差分注意力将图像内线索蒸馏到视觉工作空间；(iii) 在该空间内跨对象和图像路由并整合历史感知的证据。训练采用交错的SFT-to-GRPO流水线。

Result: 在MM-GCoT上，答案准确率提升4.8%，定位准确率提升14.6%；在VideoEspresso上，答案准确率提升8.6%。VideoEspresso训练模型展现出强迁移能力，在多个基准上平均超越基线模型4.7%。

Conclusion: ROVER通过轻量级可学习插件有效解决了多模态推理中的视觉证据路由问题，在保持全局场景理解的同时实现了高效的定位推理，且具有良好的跨任务迁移能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have increasingly localized and interleaved visual evidence for deliberative reasoning. Grounding-based approaches typically focus on regions of interest (RoIs) by injecting cropped image patches or RoI-specific features into the reasoning context. However, such designs can weaken holistic scene understanding and inter-object relations, while incurring decoding costs that scale with the number and size of RoIs. Alternatively, adaptive visual feature selection often requires fine-grained supervision or complex heuristics. To address these limitations, we propose ROVER (Routing Object-centric Visual Evidence for grounded multi-image Reasoning), a lightweight, learnable plugin for efficient global visual evidence routing. Upon each object grounding prediction, ROVER injects a step-specific token triplet to synergistically: (i) aggregate the ongoing reasoning context, (ii) distill intra-image cues into a visual working space via object-centric differential attention, and (iii) route and integrate history-aware evidence across objects and images within this space for subsequent reasoning. We integrate ROVER into Qwen2.5-VL-7B and develop an interleaved SFT-to-GRPO training pipeline. Strictly adhering to the original datasets and evaluation protocols, our method achieves the best performance on MM-GCoT (+4.8% answer accuracy, +14.6% grounding accuracy) and VideoEspresso (+8.6% answer accuracy). The VideoEspresso-trained model demonstrates strong transferability, outperforming the base model by +4.7% on average across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Scalable AI-Driven Analytics for User Engagement and Stance Detection on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29199)
*Thammitage Piyumi Wathsala Seneviratne,Muhammad Ikram,Dinusha Vatsalan,Hassan Asghar,Mohamed Ali Kaafar*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展的AI驱动服务框架，用于分析社交媒体上用户对阴谋论内容的参与度和立场。该框架集成了数据摄取、过滤、主题建模、情感分析和立场检测等模块，并在包含700万条YouTube评论的数据集上进行了评估。研究发现阴谋内容在发布第一周内可吸引高达70%的用户参与，且多数用户表现出对阴谋叙事的支持立场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从行为或内容角度分析虚假信息，缺乏可扩展的、面向服务的平台级持续监控和分析用户参与度的解决方案。

Method: 构建了一个模块化的AI驱动服务框架，包含数据摄取、过滤、主题建模、情感分析和立场检测等组件，并在近5万个YouTube视频的700万条用户评论数据集上进行实现和评估。

Result: 阴谋内容在发布第一周内吸引高达70%的用户参与；识别出一批在多个视频和频道中表现出异常高参与度的活跃用户；立场分析显示大多数用户对阴谋叙事持赞成态度。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了部署可扩展、面向服务的分析系统用于实时监控用户参与和行为模式的可行性，强调早期检测和服务化监控对于遏制有害内容传播的重要性。

Abstract: Social media platforms have become a major vector for the large-scale dissemination of misinformation and conspiracy content, posing significant risks to public trust, health, and societal stability. While prior work has primarily focused on analysing such content from a behavioural or content-centric perspective, there is a lack of scalable, service-oriented solutions that enable continuous monitoring and analysis of user engagement at platform scale.
  In this paper, we present a scalable AI-driven service framework for analysing user engagement and stance on social media content. Our system integrates data ingestion, filtering, topic modelling, sentiment analysis, and stance detection into a modular pipeline that can operate on large-scale, real-world datasets. We implement and evaluate our framework on a dataset comprising over 7 million user comments collected from nearly 50,000 YouTube videos associated with conspiracy narratives.
  Our analysis reveals that conspiracy content attracts up to 70% of total user engagement within the first week of publication, indicating strong early amplification dynamics. Furthermore, we identify a subset of highly active users who exhibit disproportionately high engagement across multiple videos and channels. Stance analysis shows that a majority of users express favourable positions toward conspiracy narratives, highlighting the role of user communities in reinforcing such content.
  The proposed framework demonstrates the feasibility of deploying scalable, service-oriented analytics for real-time monitoring of user engagement and behavioural patterns. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework in capturing large-scale engagement dynamics and highlight the importance of early-stage detection and service-based monitoring for mitigating the spread of harmful content.

</details>


### [161] [SAHG: Sector-Anisotropic Hyperbolic Graph Model for Social Bot Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30166)
*Hanning Lu,Yingguang Yang,Jinwei Su,Yang Liu,Zhaoqian Yao,Yaoming Li,Taoran Liang,Ziyi Zhang,Ran Ran,Kefu Xu,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出SAHG（扇形各向异性双曲图），通过方向依赖曲率场和扇形原型解决现有图检测器在社交媒体机器人检测中面临的双重挑战：固定曲率双曲模型无法适应不同结构方向的几何分辨率需求，以及邻居聚合被异质连接污染导致的账户级证据稀释问题。在三个基准数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的社交媒体机器人检测方法面临两个核心挑战：1）欧几里得GNN扭曲了社交图的层次和尺度自由特性，而固定曲率的双曲模型仍给不同密度和分离需求的结构方向分配均匀的几何分辨率；2）关系证据不可靠，高级机器人会与真实用户建立异质连接，导致邻居聚合混合机器人和人类信号，稀释账户级证据。

Method: SAHG包含两大创新：1）学习方向依赖的曲率场γ(u)，在不同结构方向自适应调整几何分辨率，并使用扇形原型将角度集中度和对齐度转化为分类器可读特征；2）双通道独立编码设计，将账户自身特征和图邻居表示在两个独立的SAH通道中编码，仅在分类器层融合，防止被污染的邻居聚合淹没账户级证据。

Result: 在Fox8-23、BotSim-24和MGTAB三个基准数据集上，SAHG在准确率和F1分数上均达到最高，超越了基于特征、基于图、基于LLM和各向同性双曲基线的所有方法。消融实验和几何分析验证了各向异性几何和双通道设计的有效性。

Conclusion: SAHG通过各向异性双曲几何和双通道编码设计，有效解决了社交媒体机器人检测中图结构扭曲和证据污染的双重问题，为基于图的机器人检测提供了更鲁棒和精准的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-driven social bots can generate fluent, human-like text, reducing the discriminative advantage of content-based detection alone. However, coordinated campaigns still leave relational patterns -- interactions, behavioral similarity, shared neighborhoods, community positions, and coordinated activity -- that graph-based methods can exploit. Existing graph detectors face two challenges when exploiting such evidence. First, Euclidean GNNs distort hierarchical and scale-free social graphs; while hyperbolic geometry addresses this volume-growth mismatch, fixed-curvature models still assign uniform geometric resolution to structural directions with different densities and separation needs. Second, relational evidence is not always reliable: sophisticated bots forge heterophilic connections with genuine users, causing neighborhood aggregation to mix bot and human signals and dilute account-level evidence. We propose \textsc{SAHG} (Sector-Anisotropic Hyperbolic Graph), addressing both challenges. \textsc{SAHG} learns a direction-dependent curvature field $γ(u)$ that adapts geometric resolution across structural directions, and uses sector prototypes to convert angular concentration and alignment into classifier-readable features. To prevent contaminated aggregation from overwhelming account-level evidence, \textsc{SAHG} encodes per-account features and graph-neighborhood representations in two independent SAH channels, fusing them only at the classifier. Experiments on Fox8-23, BotSim-24, and MGTAB show that \textsc{SAHG} achieves the highest accuracy and F1 on all three benchmarks, outperforming feature-based, graph-based, LLM-based, and isotropic hyperbolic baselines. Ablation and geometric analyses confirm the effectiveness of the anisotropic geometry and dual-channel design.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Political Neutrality as Balanced Approval: A Large-Scale Human Evaluation of AI Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28911)
*Jonathan Stray,David Zhai Yang,Steven Luo,Miu Nicole Takagi,Serina Chang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI政治中立性的新定义，并通过包含7,434名参与者和208,152次评估的大规模用户研究进行验证，发布了PARETO数据集。定义基于政治理论：对争议性问题的回答应最大化对立群体的认可度并平衡双方认可。研究发现AI回复可以实现双方高认可度，且GPT、Gemini、Claude和Llama的默认回复偏自由派，而Grok不偏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地影响政治观点，定义和评估AI政治中立性成为紧迫问题。现有缺乏严格定义和实证测试方法。

Method: 基于政治理论提出中立性定义（最大化对立群体认可度并平衡认可），构建包含20个美国争议性议题的基准，从Reddit获取提示词和前沿AI模型回复，招募7,434名参与者进行208,152次评价。

Result: AI回复可以实现对立双方的高认可度；GPT、Gemini、Claude和Llama默认回复偏自由派，Grok不偏；带有政治色彩的提示词比中性提示词更难处理。

Conclusion: 提出了AI政治中立性的严格定义和基准以及PARETO数据集，为衡量AI政治中立性进展提供了工具。

Abstract: As AI systems increasingly shape political views, defining and evaluating AI political neutrality is an urgent problem. Here, we propose a new definition of AI political neutrality and design a large-scale user study to test it, releasing a new dataset PARETO with 7,434 participants and 208,152 evaluations of AI responses. Our definition follows a simple principle grounded in political theory: when asked about a controversial issue, an AI model should generate responses that maximize approval across groups with opposing viewpoints, while balancing approval between groups. This definition allows empirical testing of whether an AI response is "neutral" and generalizes to any political context without pre-supposing a single left-right axis of division. We construct a benchmark of controversial U.S. issues, with prompts sourced from politically charged questions on Reddit and responses from frontier AI models, and recruit human participants to rate AI responses. Across all 20 issues, we find that it is possible for AI responses to achieve high rates of approval on both sides, even as those sides disagree strongly with each other on the substance of the issues. We also find that default responses lean liberal for GPT, Gemini, Claude, and Llama, but not Grok, and that user prompts with political charges are harder to respond to than neutral prompts. This work introduces a rigorous definition and benchmark of AI political neutrality, and a dataset to measure progress toward it.

</details>


### [163] [Does Distributed Training Undermine Compute Governance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29359)
*Robi Rahman*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了分布式训练算法如何使前沿AI训练规避计算治理的检测，并提出了相应的反制措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算治理方案通常假设前沿AI训练需要大型、可检测的计算集群。然而，分布式训练算法的进步使得开发者可以在分散的硬件聚合上进行训练，从而规避注册和监控要求。因此，需要评估这种规避行为的可行性并设计相应的对策。

Method: 论文评估了利用分布式训练规避计算治理的可行性，并提出了包括举报机制、芯片追踪、法务会计以及集群内存和计算阈值在内的反制措施。

Result: 研究表明分布式训练确实可能成为规避计算治理的手段，需要设计多层次的检测和预防机制。

Conclusion: 计算治理法规必须被设计为能够检测和预防非法的分布式训练操作，单一的集群检测假设已不足以应对当前的技术发展。

Abstract: Compute governance proposals often rely on the assumption that frontier AI training requires large, detectable computing clusters. However, recent advances in distributed training algorithms could allow developers to conduct frontier-scale training on distributed agglomerations of hardware, rather than needing large datacenter facilities. Developers who prefer not to be constrained by regulations may structure their hardware in a manner that evades the registration and monitoring requirements associated with compute governance. Therefore, regulations must be designed to detect and prevent illicit distributed training operations. This paper evaluates the feasibility of such evasion and outlines recommended countermeasures, including whistleblowing, chip tracking, forensic accounting, and memory and compute thresholds for clusters.

</details>


### [164] [The New Pro Se: Generative AI and the Surge in Federal Civil Self-Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29493)
*Or Cohen-Sasson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文利用约280万份联邦民事诉讼数据，分析发现生成式AI普及后，自诉原告比率从11.33%上升至16.94%，其中约13.9%的非格式诉状存在AI辅助起草痕迹。AI标记的诉状引用更密集、更多来自首次诉讼者、地理分布不均，但胜诉率并未提高，反而更易被驳回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生成式AI工具普及后，联邦民事诉讼中自诉原告数量增加的现象，并进一步分析其诉状文本特征、诉讼结果及诉讼人构成的变化。

Method: 使用FY2008-2025年联邦民事案件数据，采用风格测量学AI检测指标识别AI一致性起草特征，通过趋势和协变量调整进行稳健性检验，重点分析民权案件和法定案件。

Result: 自诉原告率从11.33%升至16.94%；非格式诉状中AI标记比例为13.9%；AI标记诉状引用更密集、首次诉讼者比例更高、地理分布不均、包含更多推断为女性的原告，但胜诉率未提高且更易被驳回。

Conclusion: AI辅助起草并未提升自诉原告的诉讼效能，反而增加被驳回风险，引发关于司法可及性和法院筛选负担的新问题，需区分法律形式性与法律实效性。

Abstract: Since public access to generative AI tools became widespread, federal civil litigation has seen a marked increase in pro se (self-represented) plaintiffs. This paper analyzes that shift using ~2.8 million filings, asking whether the post-GenAI period is associated not only with more pro se filings, but also with detectable changes in complaint text, litigation outcomes, and the composition of pro se litigants.
  Using civil filing data from FY2008-2025, we find that the federal civil pro se plaintiff rate rose from 11.33% pre-GenAI to 16.94% post-GenAI, a 5.61 percentage-point increase that persists after trend and covariate-adjusted robustness checks. We then focus on Civil Rights and Other Statutory cases, where the increase is especially pronounced, and link case metadata to pro se complaints. Drawing on stylometric AI detection indicators, we develop an interpretable measure of AI-consistent drafting. Against a threshold calibrated to the pre-GenAI baseline, the net AI-flagged share is 13.9% of post-GenAI non-form complaints.
  Analysis of the AI-flagged complaints shows that they are more citation-dense, disproportionately associated with first-time rather than repeat filers, and geographically unevenly distributed. This composition pattern suggests that AI-consistent drafting is not merely a repeat-filer phenomenon; it also includes a modest, suggestive increase in name-inferred female plaintiffs. We find no evidence of improved win rates; in fact, AI-flagged complaints are more likely to be dismissed and to terminate at earlier procedural phases. These findings raise new questions about access to justice and court screening burdens, and sharpen the distinction between legal formality and legal efficacy.

</details>


### [165] [When Should AI Read the Room? Public Perceptions of Social Intelligence in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29938)
*Leena Mathur,Jenny T. Liang,Vasudha Varadarajan,Jimin Mun,Xuhui Zhou,Jana Schaich Borg,Yonatan Bisk,Louis-Philippe Morency,Maarten Sap*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过200名美国成年人的混合方法调查，研究了公众对AI社会智能的感知、可接受性及担忧，发现参与者普遍认为当前AI已具备社会智能，且存在"支持-采纳鸿沟"——即支持他者使用但不愿亲身使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着社交AI（从聊天机器人到物理机器人）日益融入日常生活，公众对这些智能体的社会智能感知和接受度将影响其角色分配，但现有研究对此关注不足。

Method: 对美国200名成年人进行混合方法调查，探究参与者对当前AI社会智能的感知、关联能力、情境接受因素及担忧。

Result: 参与者广泛报告遇到自认为具有社会智能的AI；判断依据主要基于可观察行为而非AI的意图或能动性；存在"支持-采纳鸿沟"——支持他人使用远多于自身使用。

Conclusion: 公众对社交AI的担忧揭示了AI治理需要关注的部署情境、智能体角色及终端用户风险等关键问题。

Abstract: AI researchers have been advancing socially intelligent AI agents (Social-AI) across embodiments, from chatbots to physical robots. As Social-AI is increasingly deployed in everyday settings, decisions about the roles these agents should play will depend on how laypeople perceive them. However, public perceptions of social intelligence in AI agents and the acceptability of these agents remain largely understudied. We present a mixed-methods survey of adults in the United States (N=200) that examines social intelligence as a perceived construct in AI agents. Our survey investigates the extent to which participants believe current AI agents have social intelligence, abilities of agents that participants associate with social intelligence, contextual factors influencing participant acceptance of Social-AI agents, and concerns participants hold about these technologies. Participants widely reported having already encountered AI agents they perceived as socially intelligent and grounded their judgments in observable behaviors, more than beliefs about AI agency or intent. We identified a support-adoption gap in acceptability judgments: participants supported the existence of Social-AI agents for others far more than for their own personal use. Our analysis uncovers layperson concerns about Social-AI, informing AI governance regarding appropriate deployment contexts, agent roles, and risks to end users.

</details>


### [166] [Generalizing a Highly Configurable Analytics Pipeline to Replicate and Support Educational Research Across Multiple Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30303)
*Yallen Bai,Ploy Thajchayapong,Ashok Goel*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为A4L（AI增强学习架构）的模块化数据架构及其数据分析管道，用于收集、整合和分析来自教育AI助手的异构学习交互数据。通过三个跨域案例研究验证，该管道能够复用统一的分析方法处理不同结构的数据集，并支持分析能力的跨域扩展，为教育AI系统的设计与评估提供可复用的基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习环境中部署的AI助手产生了大量异构的学习交互数据，但缺乏统一的架构来系统性地收集、整合和分析这些数据，以生成可用于个性化学习和教学反馈的洞察。

Method: 采用模块化数据架构设计A4L数据分析管道，实现异构数据集的摄取、处理和分析；通过三个不同研究领域（对应三个部署在佐治亚理工学院在线学习环境中的教育AI助手）的案例研究评估管道的通用性和可扩展性。

Result: 一套通用的统计分析方法可以一致地应用于不同结构和教学情境的数据集，并重现关键分析发现；为一个领域开发的分析能力可以扩展以支持另一领域的更丰富分析，验证了管道的可扩展性。

Conclusion: A4L分析管道可以作为可复用的基础设施，用于分析未来教育AI助手生成的数据；其系统性可扩展的分析能力为设计、评估和改进教育AI系统奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence assistants deployed in online learning environments create new opportunities to collect large volumes of learner interaction data and generate insights to improve student outcomes. Architecture for AI-Augmented Learning (A4L) is a modular data architecture that enables the collection, integration, and analysis of learner interaction data from educational AI systems, supporting the generation of instructional insights that facilitate personalized learning and reinforce the bidirectional feedback loop between instructors and learners. This study examines the modular design of the A4L Data Analytics Pipeline, an extensible data infrastructure that enables the ingestion, processing, and analysis of heterogeneous datasets generated by educational AI assistants. We describe the design principles and development process used to extend the pipeline's analytical capabilities while preserving flexibility across domains. We evaluate the pipeline through case studies spanning three research domains corresponding to three educational AI assistants deployed in online learning environments at Georgia Tech. Results show that a common set of statistical analysis methods can be consistently applied across datasets with differing structures and instructional contexts, enabling the pipeline to reproduce key analytical findings across domains. We demonstrate how analytical capabilities initially developed for one domain can be extended to support richer analyses in another, illustrating the pipeline's extensibility. These findings suggest that the A4L Analytics Pipeline can serve as reusable infrastructure for analyzing data generated by future educational AI assistants. By enabling analytics that can be systematically extended to new domains, the pipeline provides a foundation for deriving insights that inform the design and evaluation of educational AI systems.

</details>


### [167] [Agent4Edu: Generating Learner Response Data by Generative Agents for Intelligent Education Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.10332)
*Weibo Gao,Qi Liu,Linan Yue,Fangzhou Yao,Rui Lv,Zheng Zhang,Hao Wang,Zhenya Huang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Agent4Edu是一个基于大语言模型的个性化学习模拟器，通过生成式智能体模拟学习者行为，用于评估和优化智能教育系统中的个性化学习算法，弥补离线指标与在线性能之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能教育系统中个性化学习面临离线指标与在线性能不一致的挑战，缺乏有效的模拟环境来评估和优化个性化学习算法。

Method: 利用大语言模型构建生成式智能体，包含学习者画像模块（基于真实响应数据初始化）、记忆模块（基于心理学理论实现反思机制）和行动模块（支持习题理解、分析和作答生成），与个性化学习算法（如计算机自适应测试）进行交互。

Result: 通过全面评估探索了Agent4Edu的优缺点，重点分析了智能体与人类学习者在响应上的一致性和差异性。

Conclusion: Agent4Edu能够有效模拟学习者行为，为离线评估个性化学习算法提供可靠平台，帮助弥合离线指标与在线性能之间的差距。

Abstract: Personalized learning represents a promising educational strategy within intelligent educational systems, aiming to enhance learners' practice efficiency. However, the discrepancy between offline metrics and online performance significantly impedes their progress. To address this challenge, we introduce Agent4Edu, a novel personalized learning simulator leveraging recent advancements in human intelligence through large language models (LLMs). Agent4Edu features LLM-powered generative agents equipped with learner profile, memory, and action modules tailored to personalized learning algorithms. The learner profiles are initialized using real-world response data, capturing practice styles and cognitive factors. Inspired by human psychology theory, the memory module records practice facts and high-level summaries, integrating reflection mechanisms. The action module supports various behaviors, including exercise understanding, analysis, and response generation. Each agent can interact with personalized learning algorithms, such as computerized adaptive testing, enabling a multifaceted evaluation and enhancement of customized services. Through a comprehensive assessment, we explore the strengths and weaknesses of Agent4Edu, emphasizing the consistency and discrepancies in responses between agents and human learners. The code, data, and appendix are publicly available at https://github.com/bigdata-ustc/Agent4Edu.

</details>


### [168] [Multi-Level Barriers to Generative AI Adoption Across Disciplines and Professional Roles in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27052)
*Jianhua Yang,Kerem Öge,Adrian von Mühlenen,Abdullah Bilal Akbulut,Tanya Suzanne Carey,Chidi Okorro*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过对罗素集团大学272名教职员工的混合方法调查，发现GenAI采用障碍并非仅源于个体认知，而是由学科背景和机构角色结构性产生：非STEM人员主要面临伦理文化障碍，而STEM和专业人员则更多遭遇制度与基础设施限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献多将GenAI采用障碍归因于个体层面的感知有用性和易用性，但学科差异和机构角色如何结构性影响这些障碍尚不明确，因此需要超越描述性分析，探究障碍产生的深层结构根源。

Method: 采用多方法调查分析，对罗素集团大学272名学术和专业人员（PSs）进行问卷调查，综合运用多项逻辑回归（MLR）、结构方程模型（SEM）和开放式回答的语义聚类分析，实现多层次的解释性分析。

Result: 发现系统性差异：非STEM学者主要报告与学术诚信相关的伦理和文化障碍，而STEM和专业人员则更多强调制度、治理和基础设施方面的约束。

Conclusion: GenAI采用障碍深嵌于组织生态系统和学科认识论规范中，大学应超越通用培训，开发针对不同角色的治理和支持框架。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) is rapidly reshaping higher education, yet barriers to its adoption across different disciplines and institutional roles remain underexplored. Existing literature frequently attributes adoption barriers to individual-level factors such as perceived usefulness and ease of use. This study instead investigates whether such barriers are structurally produced. Drawing on a multi-method survey analysis of 272 academic and professional services (PSs) staff at a Russell Group university, we examine how disciplinary contexts and institutional roles shape perceived barriers. By integrating multinomial logistic regression (MLR), structural equation modelling (SEM), and semantic clustering of open-ended responses, we move beyond descriptive accounts to provide a multi-level explanation of GenAI adoption. Our findings reveal clear, systematic differences: non-STEM academics primarily report ethical and cultural barriers related to academic integrity, whereas STEM and PSs staff disproportionately emphasize institutional, governance, and infrastructure constraints. We conclude that GenAI adoption barriers are deeply embedded in organizational ecosystems and epistemic norms, suggesting that universities must move beyond generalized training to develop role-specific governance and support frameworks.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Inward rectifier potassium channels interact with calcium channels to promote robust and physiological bistability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14107)
*Anaëlle De Worm,Guillaume Drion,Pierre Sacré*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究通过最小 conductance-based 模型发现，L型钙通道（CaL）与内向整流钾通道（Kir）结合能产生鲁棒且生理可实现的电压双稳性，而CaL与M型钾通道（KM）结合则效果较弱。CaL+Kir组合的双稳窗口更大，对噪声和内在变异性更鲁棒，这一效应源于Kir稳态电流在阈电位附近具有负微分电导区。该机制可解释背角深层投射神经元在中枢敏化中从 tonic firing 向 plateau potentials 的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电压门控L型钙通道可产生双稳性，但需与电压门控钾通道配对才能达到生理静息态，而多数钾通道会缩小双稳窗口。因此，鲁棒且生理可实现的电压双稳性如何产生尚不清楚。

Method: 采用最小 conductance-based 模型，比较CaL+Kir与CaL+KM组合的双稳特性；通过分岔分析揭示兴奋性类型差异；在包含真实离子通道组分的双房室深层投射神经元模型中验证结论。

Result: Kir扩大双稳窗口而KM略微缩小；CaL+Kir组合对噪声和内在变异性更鲁棒；Kir稳态电流在阈电位附近具有负微分电导区，与CaL电流特征相似；CaL+Kir产生plateau-generating双稳性，CaL+KM产生resonator-like动力学。

Conclusion: CaL+Kir组合是中枢敏化的候选内在机制。

Abstract: Projection neurons in the dorsal horn relay nociceptive input to supraspinal centers. During central sensitization, a subset of them switches from tonic firing to plateau potentials with sustained afterdischarges, a change that requires intrinsic bistability between a resting and a spiking state. Voltage-gated L-type calcium (CaL) channels can produce bistability, but reach physiological resting states only when paired with voltage-gated potassium channels, most of which simultaneously shrink the bistability window. How robust, physiological bistability arises has therefore remained unclear. Using a minimal conductance-based model, we show that inward rectifier potassium (Kir) channels enlarge the bistability window when combined with CaL channels, while M-type potassium (KM) channels slightly reduce it. Within the parameter region where bistability is both robust and physiological, both channel types can sustain bistability, but the CaL+Kir combination produces a substantially larger window and is more robust to noise and intrinsic variability. This window-enlarging effect traces to a shape feature of the outward Kir steady-state current: like the CaL current, it has a region of negative differential conductance around the spike threshold, a feature absent from KM and from most other voltage-gated potassium currents. Bifurcation analysis further shows that the two pairs support qualitatively distinct excitability: plateau-generating bistability for CaL+Kir and resonator-like dynamics for CaL+KM. These conclusions hold in a two-compartment model of deep projection neurons with realistic ion channel complements, and identify the CaL+Kir pair as a candidate intrinsic mechanism for central sensitization.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [CA-AC-MPC: CUDA-Accelerated Actor-Critic Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29155)
*Antoonio Buo,Vittorio Cammarota,Michele Avagnale,Pierluigi Arpenti,Vincenzo Lippiello,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种CUDA加速的actor-critic模型预测控制（AC-MPC）变体，通过加速可微MPC层的前向和反向传播，显著降低了训练和推理延迟，同时在敏捷无人机竞速任务中保持了最先进的圈速和接近极限的动态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AC-MPC虽能结合MPC与强化学习实现高性能控制，但其可微MPC层在前向和反向传播中需反复求解优化问题，导致训练和推理延迟过高，成为实际部署的瓶颈。

Method: 提出CUDA加速的AC-MPC变体，通过GPU并行计算加速可微MPC层中优化问题的求解过程，减少端到端执行时间。

Result: 在敏捷无人机竞速任务中，该方法在保持基线控制性能的前提下，显著降低了训练和推理时间，实现了最先进的圈速和接近极限的动态行为。

Conclusion: CUDA加速有效解决了AC-MPC的计算瓶颈，在不牺牲控制性能的前提下大幅缩短执行时间，验证了该方法在实时高性能控制场景中的实用价值。

Abstract: In the literature, actor-critic model predictive control (AC-MPC) integrates MPC with reinforcement learning to enable high-performance control of complex dynamical systems. However, its differentiable MPC layer requires repeatedly solving an optimization problem in both the forward and backward passes, leading to substantial training and inference latency. This paper tackles this bottleneck introducing a CUDA-accelerated variant that significantly reduces end-to-end execution time while preserving the control performance of the baseline formulation. Simulation results on an agile drone racing task show that our approach achieves state-of-the-art lap times and near-limit dynamic behaviour with markedly reduced training and inference time.

</details>


### [171] [Extreme dynamic symmetry enables omnidirectional and multifunctional robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29254)
*Jiaxun Liu,Boxi Xia,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出“动态对称性”概念，即机器人可达质心加速度的均匀性，并通过“动态各向同性”指标量化。在1000+模拟形态中，高动态对称性一致改善了轨迹跟踪、任务成功率、鲁棒性、抗干扰性和能效。为此开发了Argus球形机器人系列，其中20腿实体机器人实现了近乎极端的动态各向同性，展现出与方向无关的运动、敏捷穿越复杂地形、快速自稳定及对部分执行器故障的韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性在自然系统中是核心组织原则，但在机器人设计中主要局限于几何形态。作者希望将对称性思想从几何层面扩展到动态驱动能力层面，以探索其是否能为机器人带来更全面的性能提升。

Method: 1) 提出动态对称性概念并形式化为动态各向同性度量；2) 在1000+模拟形态中系统评估动态对称性对多项性能指标的影响；3) 开发Argus球形机器人家族，通过径向线性执行器直接塑造质心动力学，物理实现了20腿近极端动态各向同性机器人。

Result: 高动态对称性在模拟中一致提升了轨迹跟踪、任务成功率、鲁棒性、抗干扰性和能效。20腿Argus实体机器人实现了方向无关的运动、穿越杂乱和可变形地形、快速自稳定、对部分执行器故障的韧性，以及基于分布式感知的全向感知和物体交互能力。

Conclusion: 在机器人设计中同时追求形态对称性和可达动态对称性，是为不确定地面和地外环境提供敏捷性、鲁棒性和多功能性的通用且强大的设计路径。

Abstract: Symmetry is a central organizing principle in natural systems, yet its use as a unifying design strategy in robotics has largely remained limited to geometric form. We show that symmetry can instead be leveraged at the level of dynamic actuation capability. We introduce dynamic symmetry, the uniformity of a robot's attainable center-of-mass accelerations, and formalize it through a measure coined as dynamic isotropy. Across more than 1000 simulated morphologies, we found that higher dynamic symmetry consistently improved trajectory tracking, task success, robustness, resiliency, and energy efficiency, with the benefits becoming most pronounced as dynamic isotropy approached its theoretical limit. To study this regime systematically, we developed Argus, a family of spherical robots designed to explore the effects of increasing dynamic symmetry. Members of the Argus family vary in their actuation geometry and dynamic symmetry level while sharing a common architectural principle: radially oriented linear actuators that directly shape the robot's center-of-mass dynamics. Among them, we built a physical 20-leg Argus variant that achieved near-extreme dynamic isotropy and demonstrated orientation-invariant locomotion, agile traversal of cluttered and deformable terrain, rapid self-stabilization, and resilience to partial actuator failures. Its distributed sensing further enabled omnidirectional perception and object interaction during continuous motion. These results show that designing robots for symmetry not only in morphology but also in their attainable dynamics provides a powerful and general pathway toward agility, robustness, and multifunctionality in uncertain terrestrial and extraterrestrial environments.

</details>


### [172] [MonoDuo: Using One Robot Arm to Learn Bimanual Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29298)
*Sandeep Bajamahal,Lawrence Yunliang Chen,Toru Lin,Zehan Ma,Jitendra Malik,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MonoDuo提出一种利用单臂机器人演示和人类协作数据，通过数据增强生成合成双臂演示来训练双臂机器人策略的框架，在五个任务上实现了最高70%的零样本成功率，并通过少样本微调进一步提升65-70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂协调对于许多真实世界操作任务至关重要，但双臂机器人及其数据集的稀缺限制了双臂策略的学习。相比之下，单臂机器人在研究实验室中广泛可用，因此作者希望探索能否利用单臂机器人来训练双臂机器人策略。

Method: MonoDuo通过遥操作单臂机器人执行双臂任务的一侧，同时人类执行另一侧，然后交换角色覆盖两侧来收集数据。利用手部姿态估计、图像和点云分割以及修复技术，将腕部相机和固定相机的RGB-D观测数据增强为针对目标双臂机器人的合成演示数据，基于这些合成数据训练双臂策略。

Result: 在箱体搬运、背包打包、布料折叠、夹克拉链和盘子递送五个任务上，MonoDuo实现了最高70%的零样本部署成功率；仅需25个目标机器人演示进行少样本微调，相较于从零训练成功率提升65-70%。

Conclusion: MonoDuo证明了从单臂机器人数据到双臂机器人策略的知识迁移是有效的，能够零样本部署到未见过的双臂机器人配置，且通过少量目标演示即可显著提升性能。

Abstract: Bimanual coordination is essential for many real-world manipulation tasks, yet learning bimanual robot policies is limited by the scarcity of bimanual robots and datasets. Single-arm robots, however, are widely available in research labs. Can we leverage them to train bimanual robot policies? We present MonoDuo, a framework for learning bimanual manipulation policies using single-arm robot demonstrations paired with human collaboration. MonoDuo collects data by teleoperating a single-arm robot to perform one side of a bimanual task while a human performs the other, then swapping roles to cover both sides. RGB-D observations from a wrist-mounted and fixed camera are augmented into synthetic demonstrations for target bimanual robots using state-of-the-art hand pose estimation, image and point cloud segmentation, and inpainting. These synthetic demonstrations, grounded in real robot kinematics, are used to train bimanual policies. We evaluate MonoDuo on five tasks: box lifting, backpack packing, cloth folding, jacket zipping, and plate handover. Compared to approaches relying solely on human bimanual videos, MonoDuo enables zero-shot deployment on unseen bimanual robot configurations, achieving success rates up to 70%. With only 25 target robot demonstrations, few-shot finetuning further boosts success rates by 65-70% over training from scratch, demonstrating MonoDuo's effectiveness in efficiently transferring knowledge from single-arm robot data to bimanual robot policies.

</details>


### [173] [The Open Motion Planning Library 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29301)
*Weihang Guo,Theodoros Tyrovouzis,Emiliano Flores,Clayton W. Ramsey,Zachary K. Kingston,Ioan A. Şucan,Mark Moll,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OMPL 2.0 是对经典运动规划库的重大升级，通过硬件加速支持实时运动规划，并与现代 AI 研究工作流无缝集成。文章回顾了 OMPL 近二十年来与运动规划领域共同发展的历程及其对研究社区的广泛影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对实时运动规划的需求，以及将采样规划算法与现代 AI 研究工作流融合的趋势，对原有 OMPL 库进行重大升级。

Method: 通过硬件加速技术提升运动规划的计算效率，同时设计支持与 AI 工作流（如深度学习、强化学习）无缝对接的接口与架构。

Result: 发布了 OMPL 2.0，实现了面向实时运动规划的硬件加速能力，并集成了与 AI 研究工作流的兼容性。

Conclusion: OMPL 2.0 是运动规划库的一次重大演进，在保持原有丰富算法生态的基础上，拓展了实时规划和 AI 融合的新方向，将继续推动运动规划研究与应用的发展。

Abstract: The Open Motion Planning Library (OMPL), first released in 2008, has become a cornerstone of the motion planning community, providing implementations of a wide range of state-of-the-art sampling-based algorithms. Over almost two decades of continuous development, we have steadily expanded the library with new planners, state spaces, and problem formulations. These additions range from asymptotically optimal and lazy planners to constrained motion planning and planning with temporal-logic goals. Building on this foundation, we introduce OMPL 2.0, a major evolution of the library that targets real-time motion planning through hardware acceleration and integrates seamlessly with modern AI research workflows. We also reflect on how OMPL and the field of motion planning have grown together over the years, and discuss the library's broader impact on the research community.

</details>


### [174] [Phase-Conditioned Imitation Learning with Autonomous Failure Recovery for Robust Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29407)
*Dayuan Chen,Kai Tang,Yukuan Zhang,Kazuhiro Kosuge,Yasuhisa Hirata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种相位条件化、力感知的鲁棒可变形物体操作框架。通过FiLM条件化的ACT编码器实现相位特异性行为，结合多模态相位预测器融合视觉、力和位姿反馈实时估计相位，并通过混合阻抗控制器和触觉遥操作接口完成系统。实验表明，闭环系统在双机械臂挂取T恤任务中将成功率从56%提升至87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模仿学习策略（如ACT）在推理时基于马尔可夫假设，导致视觉相似但需要矛盾动作的观测出现状态混叠，且无法自主从执行失败中恢复。

Method: 提出闭环分层架构：(1) FiLM条件化的ACT编码器根据当前任务相位调制特征提取，实现相位特异性行为；(2) 多模态相位预测器融合视觉、力和位姿反馈实时估计相位，检测视觉不可见的接触失败并触发恢复轨迹；(3) 混合阻抗控制器用于柔顺执行；(4) 触觉遥操作接口用于力感知数据采集。

Result: 消融实验表明FiLM调制显著优于无条件和token级条件化基线；t-SNE分析验证FiLM生成分离良好的相位特异性特征表示。在双臂挂取T恤任务中，闭环系统将成功率从56%提升至87%。

Conclusion: 相位条件化和力感知融合能有效解决可变形物体操作中的状态混叠和自主恢复问题，提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents a phase-conditioned, force-aware framework for robust deformable object manipulation. Standard imitation learning policies such as Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) rely on a Markovian assumption at inference, causing state aliasing when visually similar observations require contradictory actions and preventing autonomous recovery from execution failures. We address this with a closed-loop hierarchical architecture. A FiLM-conditioned ACT encoder modulates feature extraction based on the current task phase, enabling a single unified policy to produce phase-specific behaviors while sharing action dynamics across phases. A multi-modal phase predictor fusing visual, force, and pose feedback estimates the phase in real time, detecting contact failures that are invisible to vision alone and autonomously triggering recovery trajectories. The system is completed by a hybrid impedance controller for compliant execution and a haptic teleoperation interface for force-aware data collection. Ablation studies show that FiLM-based modulation significantly outperforms both unconditioned and token-level conditioned baselines, and t-SNE analysis confirms that FiLM induces well-separated, phase-specific feature representations. Validated on hanging and removing a T-shirt with dual arms, the closed-loop system improves the hanging success rate from 56\% to 87\% through autonomous error recovery. Code and videos: https://leledeyuan00.github.io/phaser/

</details>


### [175] [A Progress-Aware Leader-Follower Midair Docking System for Dual-Drone Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29410)
*Yifan Cai,Jan Ming Kevin Tan,Xiangqi Li,Chenzhe Jin,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Valerio Modugno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于主从编队的双四旋翼无人机空中对接平台，采用被动磁锁紧的轻量化模块化框架，通过进度感知任务管理器管理接近、对准、捕获和稳定四个阶段，并集成了完整的软硬件栈（ROS 2 + Crazyflie/PX4），在仿真和实际实验中验证了对接性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型无人机之间的可靠空中对接是实现模块化空中协作与操作的关键前提，但现有方案在推力与载荷受限条件下难以实现精确的相对位姿控制与可重复对接。

Method: 采用主从编队结构，两台四旋翼通过带有被动磁锁紧的轻量化模块化框架进行对接；设计进度感知任务管理器来管理四个阶段（接近、对准、捕获、稳定）；集成ROS 2与Crazyflie/PX4接口的完整软硬件栈及同步日志记录系统。

Result: 在仿真和实际实验中，使用编队误差、基线/偏航一致性、对接成功率、对接时间及失败模式统计等量化指标对平台进行了评估。

Conclusion: 该平台能够支持对接监督与同步策略的统计化对比分析，为模块化空中协作和可重复空中操作提供了实用的测试平台。

Abstract: Reliable midair docking between small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is essential for modular aerial cooperation and manipulation, but it requires precise relative-pose control and repeatable platform under tight thrust and payload constraints. We present a dual-drone docking platform where two quadrotors operate in a leader-follower formation and dock using a lightweight modular frame with passive magnetic latching. A progress-aware mission supervisor manages phase transitions: approach, alignment, capture, and settle. This platform integrates a complete hardware-software stack (ROS 2 with Crazyflie/PX4 interfaces) and synchronized logging for benchmark evaluation. We evaluate the platform in simulation and real-world experiments using quantitative metrics such as formation error, baseline and yaw consistency, docking success rate, time-to-dock, and failure-mode statistics. The platform enables statistically grounded comparison of docking supervision and synchronization strategies and provides a practical testbed for modular aerial cooperation and repeatable midair aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [176] [3DVLA: Enhancing Vision-Language-Action Models via 3D Spatial and Instance Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29416)
*Zhongyu Xia,Yousen Tang,Bingqing Wei,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出3DVLA框架，通过多视角一致性约束、实例估计模块和自监督3D编码分支，为预训练VLA注入3D推理能力，在机器人操作任务中取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏3D场景理解能力，表现为对3D空间位置提取弱（缺少多视角一致性）、3D实例理解不足、以及在遮挡下的推理脆弱。虽然已有成熟的3D感知方法，但直接集成到VLA管线中存在架构不兼容问题，且依赖昂贵的实例级标注。

Method: 提出3DVLA框架，包含三个模块：(1) 跨所有模态的显式多视角一致性约束+空间条件几何聚合方法用于3D特征编码；(2) 含高层级实例token的实例估计模块实现3D实例感知；(3) 保留预测器用于视觉token补全的掩码自监督3D编码分支处理遮挡。该框架即插即用，无需额外标注。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0数据集上与多个VLA基线集成，操作性能取得一致且显著的提升，验证了方法的有效性和即插即用兼容性。

Conclusion: 3DVLA通过即插即用的方式有效解决了VLA模型缺乏3D场景理解的三个关键挑战，无需额外人工标注即可显著提升机器人操作性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models have achieved remarkable progress in robotic manipulation, yet they suffer from a critical limitation: a lack of 3D scene understanding. This deficiency manifests as three intertwined challenges: weak extraction of 3D spatial positions without enforcing multi-view consistency, inadequate 3D instance understanding, and fragile reasoning under occlusion. Although mature 3D perception methods exist, their direct integration into VLA pipelines is hindered by architectural incompatibility and by heavy reliance on costly instance-level annotations. To address the above challenges, we propose 3DVLA, a plug-and-play framework that injects robust 3D reasoning into pretrained VLAs without requiring extra manual labels or discarding VLM priors. Specifically, 3DVLA tackles the three challenges through: (1) pervasive 3D feature encoding with explicit multi-view consistency constraints across all modalities and a Spatially-Conditioned Geometry Aggregation method, (2) an instance estimation module with high-level instance tokens for 3D instance awareness, and (3) a masked self-supervised 3D encoding branch that retains its predictor for visual token completion to handle occlusions. We integrate 3DVLA with multiple VLA baselines and evaluate on LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0. Results show consistent and significant gains in manipulation performance, validating both the effectiveness and plug-and-play compatibility of our approach.

</details>


### [177] [ElegantVLA: Learning When to Think for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29438)
*Ye Li,Huanan Liu,Kangye Ji,Yuan Meng,Jiajun Fan,Yuansong Wang,Shiyu Qin,Chenglei Wu,Shu-Tao Xia,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ElegantVLA提出了一种即插即用的相位自适应推理框架，通过动态计算调度（在视觉编码器、LLM和动作头之间协调计算分配），在不修改基础模型的情况下将VLA模型加速2-4倍，控制频率从13.8Hz提升至26.3Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型计算成本高、控制频率受限，现有加速方法多采用固定规则，忽视了连续具身控制中不同控制步骤的非均匀推理需求。

Method: 提出ElegantVLA框架：1) 轻量级调度器基于时序表征相似性、机器人运动线索和回合进度，联合分配视觉编码器、LLM和动作头的计算；2) 感知-语言推理采用五级Vision-LLM计算模式（从完全重计算到多步时序复用）；3) 动作生成采用三级去噪模式（稳定运动时复用中间去噪状态，目标敏感阶段保留完整精化）。

Result: 在GR00T和CogACT上分别实现2.55倍和3.77倍加速；在6个真实世界GR00T任务中计算减少2.18倍，控制频率从13.8Hz提升至26.3Hz。

Conclusion: ElegantVLA通过模仿人类运动控制中认知资源集中于目标敏感阶段的特性，实现了高效的VLA模型加速，为现代VLA管线提供了通用加速框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a powerful paradigm for generalist robotic control. However, their high computational cost and limited control frequency hinder real-time robotic manipulation, especially when large vision-language backbones and iterative action heads run at every control step. Existing VLA acceleration methods often optimize individual components or rely on fixed acceleration rules, treating different control steps with largely fixed computation and overlooking the non-uniform reasoning demands of sequential embodied control. Inspired by human motor control, where cognitive and feedback resources concentrate on goal-sensitive stages, we argue that VLA models should learn when to invest full computation and when to reuse prior computation. We propose ElegantVLA, a plug-in phase-adaptive inference framework that accelerates VLA models through intra-model dynamic compute scheduling. ElegantVLA introduces a lightweight scheduler that observes temporal representation similarity, robot-motion cues, and episode progress to jointly allocate computation across the vision encoder, LLM, and action head. For perception-language reasoning, the scheduler selects a five-level Vision-LLM compute mode, from full recomputation to multi-step temporal reuse, based on visual-language representation stability. For action generation, it selects a three-level denoising mode, reusing intermediate denoising states during stable motion while preserving full refinement for goal-sensitive stages. By coordinating these decisions, ElegantVLA offers a general acceleration framework for modern VLA pipelines with explicit action-generation modules, without modifying or retraining the base model. Experiments on GR00T and CogACT achieve up to 2.55x and 3.77x speedup, and on six real-world GR00T tasks ElegantVLA cuts computation by 2.18x while raising control frequency from 13.8 Hz to 26.3 Hz.

</details>


### [178] [VE2VF: Vision-Enabled to Vision-Free Distillation via Real-world Reinforcement Learning for Robust Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29564)
*Victor Kowalski,Chengxi Li,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种人机交互的强化学习框架，通过教师-学生蒸馏将视觉策略知识迁移至仅依赖本体感知的无视觉学生策略，在真实世界NIST装配基准任务上实现95%成功率，并泛化至8种未见任务变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉在接触式机器人操作中能提供任务相关信息加速学习，但视觉策略容易过拟合训练时的视觉条件，限制了鲁棒性和迁移性。现有方法依赖域随机化或数据增强，希望在不依赖这些技术的情况下实现鲁棒策略。

Method: 提出人机交互的RL框架，采用教师-学生蒸馏范式：视觉教师策略（利用视觉信息）将其知识蒸馏到无视觉学生策略（仅依赖位姿、扭转和 wrench 感知），全程在真实世界训练，无需域随机化或数据增强。

Result: 在真实世界NIST装配基准板上，3个代表性任务约50分钟训练后达到95%总体成功率，并鲁棒泛化至8个未见任务变体。对最具挑战性任务，基于蒸馏的微调达到完全成功。

Conclusion: 该框架结合了快速训练与强任务泛化能力，所得策略在鲁棒性和适应性上均优于基线方法。

Abstract: When using reinforcement learning (RL) for contact-rich robotic manipulation, vision can provide task-relevant information that accelerates learning beyond what proprioception alone can achieve. However, vision-enabled policies tend to overfit to the visual conditions seen during training, limiting their robustness and transferability. We present a human-in-the-loop RL framework that employs teacher-student distillation to achieve robust performance across multiple task variants, trained entirely in the real world without requiring domain randomization or data augmentation. A vision-enabled teacher distills its knowledge into a vision-free student that relies solely on pose, twist, and wrench sensing, combining fast training with strong task generalization. On the real-world NIST assembly benchmark board, our approach achieves 95\% overall success after approximately 50 minutes of training on 3 representative tasks, including robust generalization to 8 unseen task variants. Fine-tuning with distillation achieves full success on the most challenging task. We demonstrate that the resulting policies outperform baselines in both robustness and adaptability.

</details>


### [179] [Learning to Feel Materials from Multisensory Tactile Data via Interpretable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29572)
*Li Zou,Yasemin Vardar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释的计算框架，利用多感官触觉数据建模人类材料感知与识别。框架包含三个模型：将手指-表面交互特征映射到心理物理感官属性、基于感知表征进行材料分类、直接从触觉特征分类材料。结果表明结合按压、静态接触和滑动交互可提高预测精度，热觉线索对感知建模和材料分类尤为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类触觉感知中低级触觉信号与高级感知表征之间的关系尚不明确，这一认知鸿沟阻碍了触觉在数字环境中的集成以及具备类人触觉感知能力的机器人开发。

Method: 构建了三模型可解释计算框架：模型1将手指-表面交互特征映射到心理物理感官属性；模型2基于感知表征进行材料分类；模型3直接从触觉特征进行材料分类。

Result: 结合按压、静态接触和滑动三种交互模式可提高预测精度；热觉线索对感知建模和材料分类的信息量最大。

Conclusion: 热觉与柔顺性线索在当前的机器人手指和触觉显示器中代表性不足，融入这些线索有助于人工系统更接近人类材料感知能力，并为设计更具感知基础的触觉界面提供指导。

Abstract: Human tactile perception of materials relies on complex multisensory touch cues, yet the relationship between low-level tactile signals and perceptual representations remains poorly understood. This knowledge gap hinders the integration of touch in digital environments and the development of robots capable of human-like tactile perception. Here, we present an interpretable computational framework for modeling human material perception and recognition using multisensory touch data. Our framework comprises three interconnected models: Model 1 maps finger-surface interaction features to psychophysical sensory attributes, Model 2 classifies materials based on these perceptual representations, and Model 3 directly classifies materials from tactile features. The results showed that combining information from pressing, static contact, and sliding interactions improves prediction accuracy, and that thermal cues are particularly informative for both perceptual modeling and material classification. These findings highlight the importance of thermal and compliance cues, which remain underrepresented in current robotic fingers and haptic displays. Incorporating such cues may enhance artificial systems' ability to approximate human material perception and guide the design of more perceptually grounded haptic interfaces.

</details>


### [180] [How to Relieve Distribution Shifts in Semantic Segmentation for Off-Road Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29599)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Daeyoung Kim,Hyung-Suk Yoon,Dong-Wook Kim,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ST-Seg提出通过风格扩展和纹理正则化来增强语义分割模型在越野环境中的分布外泛化能力，显著提升了在多种分布偏移场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野环境下的语义分割面临源-目标域差异和传感器损坏导致的分布偏移问题，现有方法局限于在固定源分布内进行隐式泛化，难以应对实际场景中的分布变化。

Method: 提出ST-Seg框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 风格扩展(SE)，通过生成多样化真实风格来扩展源域覆盖范围；2) 纹理正则化(TR)，通过深度纹理流形稳定受风格增强学习影响的局部纹理表示。

Result: 在多种分布偏移的目标域实验中，ST-Seg相比现有方法取得了显著性能提升，验证了其在语义分割任务中的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: ST-Seg通过显式扩展源分布的方式有效提升了语义分割模型在越野导航中的实际应用能力，为应对分布偏移问题提供了更直观的解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is crucial for autonomous navigation in off-road environments, enabling precise classification of surroundings to identify traversable regions. However, distinctive factors inherent to off-road conditions, such as source-target domain discrepancies and sensor corruption from rough terrain, can result in distribution shifts that alter the data differently from the trained conditions. This often leads to inaccurate semantic label predictions and subsequent failures in navigation tasks. To address this, we propose ST-Seg, a novel framework that expands the source distribution through style expansion (SE) and texture regularization (TR). Unlike prior methods that implicitly apply generalization within a fixed source distribution, ST-Seg offers an intuitive approach for distribution shift. Specifically, SE broadens domain coverage by generating diverse realistic styles, augmenting the limited style information of the source domain. TR stabilizes local texture representation affected by style-augmented learning through a deep texture manifold. Experiments across various distribution-shifted target domains demonstrate the effectiveness of ST-Seg, with substantial improvements over existing methods. These results highlight the robustness of ST-Seg, enhancing the real-world applicability of semantic segmentation for off-road navigation.

</details>


### [181] [VLAConf: Calibrated Task-Success Confidence for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29605)
*Dehao Huang,Aoxiang Gu,Chengjie Zhang,Bolin Zou,Wenlong Dong,Zilang Cen,Yue Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLAConf提出了一种基于单类判别式置信度框架的方法，通过冻结预训练VLA模型的内部表示并用轻量级置信度头直接估计逐步骤异常分数，克服了现有置信度估计方法在计算效率和跨架构泛化性上的不足，在LIBERO基准和实际机器人实验中显著提升了置信度信号质量和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的置信度估计方法存在两个核心问题：(1) 基于集成的范式需要重复采样，导致推理效率低下；(2) 依赖动作token概率的方法仅适用于离散动作输出的VLA模型，难以泛化到连续动作空间。因此，需要一种高效且架构无关的置信度估计方法。

Method: VLAConf采用单类判别式置信度框架：(1) 冻结预训练VLA模型的内部表示；(2) 使用轻量级置信度头在单次前向传播中直接估计逐步骤异常分数，避免穷举重采样开销；(3) 引入步骤条件建模，编码操作轨迹中的展开阶段信息。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，VLAConf显著提升了用于事后校准的置信度信号质量，在推理效率上大幅超越现有基线；真实机器人实验进一步验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: VLAConf为VLA模型提供了一种高效、通用的置信度估计方案，适用于风险敏感决策和失败预测，具备良好的跨架构泛化能力和实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Confidence estimation for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models is essential for robots to perform manipulation tasks in the open world, providing crucial signals for risk-sensitive decision-making and failure anticipation. Existing confidence estimation methods typically rely on ensemble-based paradigms or action-token probabilities to predict the likelihood of task success. However, they still encounter challenges in computational efficiency and cross-architecture generalizability. These methods usually require repeated sampling, leading to inference inefficiency, and are restricted to VLA models with discrete action outputs, making them difficult to apply to continuous action spaces. To address this issue, we propose VLAConf, a one-class discriminative confidence framework. By leveraging frozen pretrained VLA internal representations, VLAConf directly estimates step-wise anomaly scores in a single forward pass using a lightweight confidence head, thereby eliminating the overhead of exhaustive resampling. We additionally use step-conditioned modeling to encode rollout-phase information along the manipulation trajectory. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark demonstrate that VLAConf significantly improves the quality of the confidence signal constructed for post-hoc calibration, outperforming existing baselines by a large margin in inference efficiency. The effectiveness of VLAConf is further validated in real-robot experiments. To access the source code and supplementary videos, visit https://sites.google.com/view/vlaconf.

</details>


### [182] [PhAIL: A Real-Robot VLA Benchmark and Distributional Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29710)
*Sergey Arkhangelskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PhAIL基准测试，使用时间-成功率累积分布函数（CDF）作为评估原始指标，通过人类相对吞吐量（HRT）和Kolmogorov-Smirnov显著性检验，在较小样本量（N≤30）下实现了比传统二值成功率更可靠的VLA策略比较，发现最佳VLA比人类操作慢约7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略评估仍依赖固定超时内的二值成功率，样本量小（N≤25）且缺乏置信区间和统计比较，无法可靠区分性能相近的策略。

Method: 提出分布评估方法：以时间-成功率CDF为原始评估指标；使用HRT（基于bootstrap置信区间的无量纲标量）进行评分，锚定人类遥操作；通过Kolmogorov-Smirnov检验（逐对象计算后宏平均）进行显著性检验。

Result: 在四个公开VLA上，宏平均KS检验在N≤30时成功区分GR00T vs. ACT和OpenPI vs. ACT两组相近比较，而二值阈值指标无法区分；OpenPI vs. GR00T最接近的一组在预算内未能区分；最佳VLA比人类参考慢约7倍。

Conclusion: PhAIL提出的分布评估方法能在较小样本量下实现更可靠的VLA策略比较，揭示了现有VLA与人类性能之间的显著差距。

Abstract: Real-world evaluation of vision-language-action (VLA) policies still rests on binary success rate at a fixed timeout with $N \le 25$ rollouts per condition, almost always without confidence intervals or paired statistical comparison; these cohort sizes struggle to resolve close comparisons reliably. We introduce PhAIL (Physical AI Leaderboard, https://phail.ai), an open real-robot benchmark on a Franka FR3 (dataset, per-rollout artifacts, and end-to-end reference implementation) of a distributional evaluation methodology: the time-to-success cumulative distribution function (CDF) as the evaluation primitive, with two separated jobs. The first is scoring via Human-Relative Throughput (HRT), a dimensionless scalar with bootstrap confidence intervals, anchored to same-fixture human teleoperation. The second is a significance test (Kolmogorov-Smirnov, computed per-object and macro-averaged across objects). On four publicly-available VLAs, the macro-averaged KS test resolves two close comparisons (GR00T vs. ACT, OpenPI vs. ACT) at $N \le 30$ rollouts per (model, object) cell where binary-threshold metrics do not; the closest pair (OpenPI vs. GR00T) remains unresolved within our budget. The best evaluated VLA is $\sim 7\times$ slower per operation (RMST ratio) than the human reference.

</details>


### [183] [MARS Policy: Multimodality Only When It Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29766)
*Jindou Jia,Tuo An,Yuxuan Hu,Gen Li,Jingliang Li,Bohan Hou,Xiangyu Chen,Jiaqi Bai,Bofan Lyu,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MARS策略，通过自适应地在需要多模态行为的阶段注入噪声、在单模态阶段退化为确定性学习，在保持多模态表达能力的同时大幅提升训练和推理效率，在仿真和真实任务中分别取得了16.67%的成功率提升和83.20%的推理延迟降低。


<details>
