In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework for multimodal contrastive finetuning, yielding closed-form solutions and a geometric decomposition for each strategy. This framework shows that self-distillation is more effective than other regularization approaches to retain the knowledge of the pretrained model. Our analysis reveals a largely overlooked limitation: standard Exponential Moving Average (EMA) teachers, widely used in robust finetuning, suffer from collapse. To solve this, we prove that a Weighted Moving Average (WMA) teacher maintains a persistent regularizing force over finite horizons and yields bias-free convergence in the task subspace while preserving orthogonal knowledge. These insights motivate **TRACER** (**T**rajectory-**R**obust **A**nchoring for **C**ontrastive **E**ncoder **R**egularization), which combines contrastive learning with WMA-guided multi-perspective distillation. Extensive experiments on CLIP finetuning demonstrate consistent OOD accuracy and calibration gains across three backbone architectures, and comprehensive ablations confirm that TRACER is both principled and robust to hyperparameter choices. Code is available at [https://github.com/HesamAsad/TRACER](https://github.com/HesamAsad/TRACER).

</details>


### [221] [A Geometric View of SRC: Learning Representations for Stable Residual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29673)
*Vangelis P. Oikonomou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何约束的表示学习方法，在不涉及SRC训练的情况下，通过优化类内自表达性、抑制类间重构路径和跨度对齐，提升基于重构推理（如SRC/OMP）的残差边距，并在图像、文本和EEG数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SRC等基于重构的推理方法依赖表示几何特性，但现有方法在训练中引入SRC（如展开或微分）导致训练-测试耦合；本文希望在严格分离训练与推理的前提下，通过几何塑造提升残差排序稳定性。

Method: 1. 提出跨度级理想化框架，定义残差边距并分析阻碍边距的几何障碍（跨度重叠、主导、通过小主角近重叠）；2. 导出理想残差边距的下界；3. 设计几何塑造目标：促进掩码类内自表达性、抑制跨类重构路径和跨类跨度对齐、防止坍塌——全程不依赖SRC残差或预测。

Result: 在COIL-100（图像）、TREC（文本）和EEG连接数据上，所有表示在固定SRC/OMP推理下评估，报告了残差边距和几何诊断指标；以交叉熵作为参考几何基线进行比较。

Conclusion: 严格的训练-推理分离可行，通过直接塑造表示几何性质可提升基于重构推理的性能，为理解SRC类方法的可靠性提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based inference assigns a class by comparing class-wise reconstruction residuals; Sparse Representation Classification (SRC) is a canonical instance whose reliability depends on the geometry of the learned representation. We adopt a strict training-inference separation: SRC is used only as a fixed test-time rule and is never differentiated, unrolled, or optimized during training. In a span-level idealization based on class-conditional spans and their associated projection residuals, we formalize residual-ordering stability through a residual margin and characterize geometric obstructions -- span overlap, dominance, and near-overlap via small principal angles -- that can collapse this margin in worst-case directions. This span-level theory is primary: it specifies when the idealized residual family is well-separated, and it provides a conditional solver-level interpretation for practical residual approximations (e.g., OMP) insofar as they remain close to the span-level residual ordering. Under explicit coverage and separation assumptions, we derive a quantitative lower bound on the (idealized) residual margin. Guided by these targets, we propose geometry-shaping objectives that promote masked within-class self-expressiveness, discourage cross-class reconstruction pathways and inter-class span alignment, and prevent collapse -- without invoking SRC residuals or predictions during training. Experiments on images (COIL-100), text (TREC), and EEG connectivity evaluate all representations under identical fixed SRC/OMP inference and report residual margins and geometric diagnostics; cross-entropy is included only as a reference geometry under the same evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [222] [Treatment-Conditioned Diffusion for Forecasting Neurodegenerative Disease Progression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29932)
*Danylo Boiko,Viktoriia Mishkurova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种治疗条件扩散框架，通过条件化生成过程（基于患者DaTscan影像和左旋多巴等效日剂量）来预测帕金森病未来脑部状态，使用Transformer编码器建模药代动力学，并利用多权重ROI掩膜聚焦关键脑区，在保持解剖细节的同时比基线方法MSE降低14.0%、MAE降低7.2%、SSIM提升4.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统通常产生标量临床评分，忽略了纵向神经影像的丰富结构；而传统生成方法则存在解剖细节丢失和模糊化进展模式的问题，无法有效预测神经退行性疾病的进展。

Method: 提出治疗条件扩散框架，以患者DaTscan影像和一年内左旋多巴等效日剂量为条件，使用Transformer编码器建模非线性药代动力学，并通过多权重区域兴趣掩膜优化生成过程，聚焦生物学关键区域。

Result: 与基线相比，MSE降低14.0%，MAE降低7.2%，SSIM提升4.9%，保持了锐利的解剖边界并显著提升了临床保真度。

Conclusion: 该扩散框架能有效预测高保真度的未来脑部状态，保持解剖细节，为帕金森病的长期规划和个性化治疗干预提供了更可靠的预测工具。

Abstract: Forecasting the progression of neurodegenerative diseases, such as Parkinson's disease, is essential for effective long-term planning and personalized therapeutic intervention. Existing systems typically produce scalar clinical scores that ignore the rich structure of longitudinal neuroimaging, while traditional generative approaches suffer from a loss of anatomical details and blurring subtle progression patterns. To address this, we introduce a novel treatment-conditioned diffusion framework that predicts high-fidelity future brain states by conditioning the generative process on patients' screening DaTscan images and levodopa equivalent daily dose over one year. The pipeline uses a Transformer-based encoder to represent non-linear, time-dependent pharmacological dynamics and optimizes generation through a multi-weight region-of-interest mask that focuses on biologically critical areas. Experimental evaluation shows that our framework maintains sharp anatomical boundaries and significantly improves clinical fidelity relative to the baseline, achieving 14.0% lower MSE, 7.2% lower MAE, and 4.9% higher SSIM.

</details>


### [223] [Improving Adversarial Robustness of Attribution via Implicit Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29983)
*Amir Mehrpanah,Matteo Gamba,Hossein Azizpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现对抗归因鲁棒性可以通过标准SGD的学习动态隐式获得，无需显式正则化；同时揭示了softmax归一化下注意力归因的固有限制，并提出用核注意力替代可恢复鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中归因的可解释性需要对抗鲁棒性，但现有方法依赖计算昂贵的显式正则化，本文探索是否可以通过学习动态隐式获得鲁棒性。

Method: 理论分析参数空间与输入空间曲率的联系，证明隐式鲁棒性；在多种架构、数据集和归因方法上实验验证；理论证明softmax归一化下注意力归因因熵约束无法传递鲁棒性，并提出核注意力替代方案。

Result: 标准SGD可隐式赋予归因鲁棒性且计算开销可忽略；softmax归一化下注意力归因无法获得类似鲁棒性；核注意力可恢复Transformer模型的鲁棒性增益。

Conclusion: 学习动态是实现鲁棒可解释性的一种有原则且实用的机制，同时揭示了归一化下注意力归因的固有限制。

Abstract: The adversarial robustness of attributions is a fundamental requirement for reliable explainability in deep learning, yet existing approaches typically rely on computationally expensive explicit regularization. In this work, we show that attribution robustness can arise implicitly from the learning dynamics of standard stochastic gradient descent. We theoretically motivate this effect through connections between parameter-space and input-space curvature, and validate it across architectures, datasets, and attribution methods, with negligible computational overhead. In contrast, we prove that such robustness gains often does not transfer to attention-based attribution under softmax normalization, due to inherent entropy constraints, and we validate this limitation experimentally. Finally, we show that replacing softmax attention with kernel-based attention restores the robustness gains in transformer models. Our results highlight learning dynamics as a principled and practical mechanism for robust explainability, and reveal fundamental limitations of attention-based attribution under normalization.

</details>


### [224] [Learning Locally, Revising Globally: Global Reviser for Federated Learning with Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00452)
*Yuxin Tian,Mouxing Yang,Yuhao Zhou,Jian Wang,Qing Ye,Tongliang Liu,Gang Niu,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中的标签噪声问题（F-LN），本文发现全局模型具有缓慢记忆噪声标签的特性，据此提出FedGR方法，包含三个模块协同纠错与正则化，在三个基准上优于八种最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习高度依赖高质量标签，但在现实场景中难以保证，导致联邦标签噪声（F-LN）问题。该问题因FL的异质性（不同客户端经历不同类型的标签噪声、比例和数据分布）而进一步加剧。

Method: 首先观察到FL全局模型对噪声标签的记忆速度较慢，能保持可靠预测和鲁棒表示。基于此，提出FedGR方法，包含三个模块协同工作：纠正噪声标签并正则化局部训练，以自包含方式提升FL对标签噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的F-LN基准数据集上，FedGR在严重标签噪声和数据异质性条件下持续优于八种最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 利用联邦学习全局模型对噪声标签缓慢记忆的内在特性，FedGR能够有效解决F-LN问题，无需外部数据或额外标注，是一种简洁且高效的方案。

Abstract: Conventional federated learning (FL) heavily depends on high-quality labels, which are often impractical in the real world, leading to the federated label-noise (F-LN) problem. Worse still, the F-LN problem is exacerbated by the heterogeneity of FL, whereas clients experience different label-noise types, ratios, and data distribution. In this study, we first observe an intriguing phenomenon that the global model of FL exhibits a slow memorization of noisy labels, suggesting its ability to maintain reliable predictions and robust representations in FL. Motivated by this, we propose a novel method termed Federated Global Reviser (\method), a straightforward yet effective method comprising three modules that collaboratively rectify noisy labels and regularize local training. By exploiting this inherent property, \method\ improves the label-noise robustness of FL in a self-contained manner. Extensive experiments on three widely used F-LN benchmarks demonstrate the superior performance of FedGR, consistently outperforming eight state-of-the-art baselines even in severe label-noise and data heterogeneity. Code: https://github.com/cs-yuxintian/FedGR-ICML26

</details>


### [225] [A Composable Multimodal Framework for cine CMR-Text-Driven Prediction of Heart Failure Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16548)
*Jianzhou Chen,Jinyang Sun,Xiumei Wang,Xi Chen,Heyu Chu,Guo Song,Yuji Luo,Xingping Zhou,Rong Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种用于心力衰竭评估和治疗优化的组合式策略框架，该框架通过整合心脏磁共振影像、结构化临床指标和非结构化文本记录等多模态数据，实现了比单模态AI算法更优的预后预测精度，并支持个性化治疗规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管心力衰竭治疗已取得显著进展，但由于其复杂性和多因素特征，仍存在大量未满足的临床需求。现有方法未能充分利用多源异构数据，亟需一种更全面的患者评估和管理方案。

Method: 构建多模态算法框架，整合cine CMR序列、结构化临床指标（如实验室结果、人口统计学数据）和非结构化文本记录（如病史、处方），进行系统性分析与预后预测。

Result: 多模态框架在心力衰竭预后预测方面显著优于单模态AI算法，并能详细评估多种病理指标对心衰结局的影响。

Conclusion: 通过系统性整合异质性临床数据，该方法支持更全面的预后评估，有助于实现心衰患者的优化、个性化治疗规划。

Abstract: Objective. Heart failure is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, with millions of deaths each year, according to data from the World Health Organization (WHO) and other public health agencies. While significant progress has been made in the field of heart failure, leading to improved survival rates and improvement of ejection fraction, there remains substantial unmet needs, due to the complexity and multifactorial characteristics. This study aims to propose and evaluate a composable strategy framework for assessment and treatment optimization in heart failure, designed to provide more holistic patient evaluation and management. Approach. The framework leverages multi-modal algorithms to analyze a comprehensive range of patient data, explicitly integrating cine cardiac magnetic resonance (cine CMR) sequences, structured clinical metrics (e.g., lab results, demographics), and unstructured textual records (e.g., medical history, prescriptions). By integrating these various data sources, our framework offers a more holistic evaluation and optimized treatment plan for patients. Main results. The multi-modal framework demonstrates superior accuracy in HF prognosis prediction compared to single-modal AI algorithms. Additionally, it enables a detailed evaluation of the impact of various pathological indicators on HF outcomes. Significance. By integrating heterogeneous clinical data in a systematic manner, this approach supports more comprehensive prognosis assessment and facilitates optimized, personalized treatment planning for heart failure patients.

</details>


### [226] [Multi-level Collaborative Distillation Meets Global Workspace Model: A Unified Framework for OCIL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08677)
*Shibin Su,Guoqiang Liang,De Cheng,Shizhou Zhang,Lingyan Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于全局工作空间理论（GWT）的在线类增量学习方法GWM，通过融合多个学生模型的参数构建共享隐式记忆，并引入多层级协同蒸馏机制，在严格记忆约束下实现了稳定性与可塑性的更好平衡，在三个标准OCIL基准上显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线类增量学习（OCIL）面临非独立同分布数据流和样本仅能观察一次的限制，导致稳定性与可塑性平衡挑战加剧；现有基于回放的方法在严格记忆缓冲区约束下效果不佳，而集成方法虽提升可塑性但稳定性不足。

Method: 受全局工作空间理论启发，提出全局工作空间模型（GWM），通过在每批训练中融合所有学生模型的参数形成共享隐式记忆，捕捉历史学习轨迹并作为知识巩固的动态锚点；GWM周期性地广播回学生模型以稳定学习并促进跨任务一致性；同时引入多层级协同蒸馏机制，在学生之间及学生与GWM之间执行一致性约束，保留历史知识。

Result: 在三个标准OCIL基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在不同记忆预算下显著提升了多种OCIL模型的性能。

Conclusion: 所提GWM方法通过全局工作空间理论和多层级蒸馏机制，有效缓解了在线类增量学习中稳定性与可塑性的矛盾，在严格记忆约束下优于现有方法。

Abstract: Online Class-Incremental Learning (OCIL) enables models to learn continuously from non-i.i.d. data streams. Since samples of the data streams can be seen only once, it is more suitable for real-world scenarios compared to offline learning. However, this constraint intensifies the challenge for OCIL in maintaining an appropriate balance between stability and plasticity. Moreover, under stricter memory buffer constraints in real world, current replay-based methods are less effective. While ensemble methods improve plasticity, they often struggle with stability. Inspired by the Global Workspace Theory (GWT), we propose a novel approach that enhances ensemble learning through a Global Workspace Model (GWM)-a shared, implicit memory that guides the learning of multiple student models. The GWM is formed by fusing the parameters of all students within each training batch, capturing the historical learning trajectory and serving as a dynamic anchor for knowledge consolidation. Like the broadcasting mechanism of GWT, the GWM is redistributed periodically to students, stabilizing learning and promoting cross-task consistency. In addition, we introduce a multi-level collaborative distillation mechanism. It enforces peer-to-peer consistency among students and preserves historical knowledge by aligning each student with the GWM. As a result, student models remain adaptable to new tasks while maintaining previously learned knowledge, striking a better balance between stability and plasticity. Extensive experiments on three standard OCIL benchmarks show that our method delivers significant performance improvement for several OCIL models across various memory budgets. The code is available at https://github.com/susususushi/GWM.

</details>


### [227] [NCSAM Noise-Compensated Sharpness-Aware Minimization for Noisy Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19947)
*Jiayu Xu,Junbiao Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从优化角度研究噪声标签学习，通过建立标签噪声与SAM平坦性偏好之间的理论联系，提出噪声补偿的尖锐感知最小化（NCSAM）方法，利用噪声补偿扰动来抵消噪声标签引起的优化偏差，从而在保持优化过程简洁性的同时减轻对噪声标签的记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据集常包含错误标注，现有的噪声标签学习方法主要依赖标签修正或样本选择机制，缺乏从优化视角系统性地处理噪声标签带来的优化偏差的研究。

Method: NCSAM通过理论分析建立了标签噪声与SAM平坦性偏好之间的关联，设计了一种噪声补偿扰动机制，该机制能够校正因噪声标签而失真的SAM扰动，从而抵消噪声标签引入的优化偏差。

Result: 在合成噪声和真实世界噪声标签基准上的实验表明，NCSAM一致优于基于SAM的优化基线，并与代表性的噪声标签学习方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 从优化角度处理噪声标签问题是可行且有效的，NCSAM通过噪声补偿的SAM变体实现了对噪声标签记忆的缓解，同时保持了优化方法的简洁性。

Abstract: Learning from Noisy Labels (LNL) remains a fundamental challenge in deep learning because real-world datasets often contain corrupted annotations. Most existing methods rely on label correction or sample selection mechanisms. In contrast, we study LNL from an optimization perspective by establishing a theoretical connection between label noise and the flatness-seeking behavior of Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM). Based on this analysis, we propose Noise-Compensated Sharpness-Aware Minimization (NCSAM), which uses a noise-compensated perturbation to counteract the optimization bias induced by noisy labels. By correcting distorted SAM perturbations, NCSAM mitigates the memorization of noisy labels during training while preserving the simplicity of optimization-based learning. Experiments on synthetic and real-world noisy-label benchmarks show that NCSAM consistently improves over SAM-based optimization baselines and remains competitive with representative noisy-label learning methods.

</details>


### [228] [Rectified LpJEPA: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures with Sparse and Maximum-Entropy Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01456)
*Yilun Kuang,Yash Dagade,Tim G. J. Rudner,Randall Balestriero,Yann LeCun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rectified Distribution Matching Regularization (RDMReg)，通过将表示对齐到Rectified Generalized Gaussian (RGG)分布，在联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)中实现稀疏、非负的表示学习，在保持任务相关性的同时获得良好的稀疏性-性能平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA方法使用各向同性高斯分布正则化表示，倾向于学习稠密表示，无法捕捉高效表示中关键的稀疏性特性。

Method: 提出RDMReg，一种切片式两样本分布匹配损失，将表示对齐到Rectified Generalized Gaussian (RGG)分布。RGG通过修正机制显式控制期望ℓ₀范数，其连续截断部分在期望ℓₚ范数和支撑集约束下具有最大熵特性。将RDMReg应用于JEPA得到Rectified LpJEPA。

Result: Rectified LpJEPA学习到稀疏、非负的表示，在图像分类基准上展现出良好的稀疏性-性能权衡，并取得有竞争力的下游性能。

Conclusion: RDMReg能够在保持任务相关信息的同时强制表示稀疏性，严格推广了先前基于高斯的JEPA方法。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) learn view-invariant representations and admit projection-based distribution matching for collapse prevention. Existing approaches regularize representations towards isotropic Gaussian distributions, but inherently favor dense representations and fail to capture the key property of sparsity observed in efficient representations. We introduce Rectified Distribution Matching Regularization (RDMReg), a sliced two-sample distribution-matching loss that aligns representations to a Rectified Generalized Gaussian (RGG) distribution. RGG enables explicit control over expected $\ell_0$ norm through rectification, while its continuous truncated component admits a maximum-entropy characterization under expected $\ell_p$ norm and support constraints. Equipping JEPAs with RDMReg yields Rectified LpJEPA, which strictly generalizes prior Gaussian-based JEPAs. Empirically, Rectified LpJEPA learns sparse, non-negative representations with favorable sparsity--performance trade-offs and competitive downstream performance on image classification benchmarks, showing that RDMReg can enforce sparsity while preserving task-relevant information.

</details>
